+class HiveService {
+  static final HiveService _instance = HiveService._internal();
+
+  factory HiveService() => _instance;
+
+  HiveService._internal();
+
+  static const String cartBoxName = 'meer_cart';
+
+  static const String favoritesBoxName = 'meer_favorites';
+
+  late final Box<dynamic> cartBox;
+
+  late final Box<dynamic> favoritesBox;
+
+  bool _initialized = false;
+
+  Future<void> init() async {
+    if (_initialized) return;
+
+    await Hive.initFlutter();
+
+    cartBox = await Hive.openBox<dynamic>(cartBoxName);
+
+    favoritesBox = await Hive.openBox<dynamic>(
+      favoritesBoxName,
+    );
+
+    _initialized = true;
+  }
+}

+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/seller_repository.dart';
+
+part 'account_event.dart';
+part 'account_state.dart';
+part 'account_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+class AccountBloc extends Bloc<AccountEvent, AccountState> {
+  AccountBloc(this._sellerRepository) : super(const AccountState.initial()) {
+    on<AccountLoadRequested>(_onLoadRequested);
+    on<AccountRefreshRequested>(_onRefreshRequested);
+  }
+
+  final SellerRepository _sellerRepository;
+
+  Future<void> _onLoadRequested(
+    AccountLoadRequested event,
+    Emitter<AccountState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    await _loadProfile(emit);
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _onRefreshRequested(
+    AccountRefreshRequested event,
+    Emitter<AccountState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    await _loadProfile(emit);
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _loadProfile(
+    Emitter<AccountState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    emit(const AccountState.loading());
+
+    final result = await _sellerRepository.getMyProfile();
+
+    result.fold(
+      (failure) {
+        emit(AccountState.error(failure.message));
+      },
+      (profile) {
+        emit(AccountState.loaded(profile: profile));
+      },
+    );
+  }
+}

+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+
+  @override

+    return BlocBuilder<AccountBloc, AccountState>(
+      builder: (context, state) {
+        return state.when(
+          initial: () => _buildLoadingScreen(),
+

+
+          loaded: (profile) {

+                },
+
+                onManageOrders: () {
+                  Navigator.pushNamed(
+                    context,
+                    AppRoute.sellerOrdersScreen,
+                  );
+                },
+
+                onSignOut: signOut,
+
+                onStartSelling: () async {
+                  await Navigator.pushNamed(
+                    context,
+                    AppRoute.sellerSetupScreen,
+                  );
+                  if (!context.mounted) return;
+
+                  // ╪¬╪¡╪»┘è╪½ ┘à┘ä┘ü ╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à ╪¿╪╣╪» ╪º┘ä╪╣┘ê╪»╪⌐ ┘à┘å ╪Ñ╪╣╪»╪º╪» ╪º┘ä┘à╪¬╪¼╪▒.
+                  context.read<AccountBloc>().add(
+                    const AccountEvent.refreshRequested(),
+                  );
+                },
+              ),
+            );
+          },
+
+          error: (message) {
+            return Scaffold(
+              appBar: AppBar(
+                title: const Text('╪¡╪│╪º╪¿┘è'),
+              ),
+              body: Center(
+                child: Padding(
+                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
+                  child: Column(
+                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
+                    children: [
+                      const Icon(
+                        Icons.error_outline,
+                        size: 48,
+                      ),
+                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
+                      Text(
+                        message,
+                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
+                      ),
+                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
+                      FilledButton.icon(
+                        onPressed: () {
+                          context.read<AccountBloc>().add(
+                            const AccountEvent.loadRequested(),
+                          );
+                        },
+                        icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
+                        label: const Text('╪Ñ╪╣╪º╪»╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¡╪º┘ê┘ä╪⌐'),
+                      ),
+                    ],
+                  ),
+                ),
+              ),
+            );
+          },
+        );
+      },
+    );
+  }
+
+  Widget _buildLoadingScreen() {

+        title: const Text('╪¡╪│╪º╪¿┘è'),

+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/cart/models/cart_item.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/local/cart_local_repository.dart';
+
+part 'cart_event.dart';
+part 'cart_state.dart';
+part 'cart_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+class CartBloc extends Bloc<CartEvent, CartState> {
+  CartBloc(
+    this._cartLocalRepository, {
+    List<CartItem> initialItems = const [],
+  }) : super(
+         CartState(items: List.unmodifiable(initialItems)),
+       ) {
+    on<CartProductAdded>(_onProductAdded);
+    on<CartQuantityIncreased>(_onQuantityIncreased);
+    on<CartQuantityDecreased>(_onQuantityDecreased);
+    on<CartProductRemoved>(_onProductRemoved);
+    on<CartCleared>(_onCleared);
+    on<CartMessageCleared>(_onMessageCleared);
+  }
+
+  final CartLocalRepository _cartLocalRepository;
+
+  /// ╪Ñ╪╢╪º┘ü╪⌐ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪Ñ┘ä┘ë ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐.
+  Future<void> _onProductAdded(
+    CartProductAdded event,
+    Emitter<CartState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    final product = event.product;
+
+    if (product.id == null) {
+      emit(
+        state.copyWith(message: '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪Ñ╪╢╪º┘ü╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼.'),
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (!product.isAvailable) {
+      emit(
+        state.copyWith(
+          message: '┘ç╪░╪º ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪║┘è╪▒ ┘à╪¬╪º╪¡ ┘ä┘ä╪¿┘è╪╣ ╪¡╪º┘ä┘è┘ï╪º.',
+        ),
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+
+    // ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘ê╪º╪¡╪»╪⌐ ╪¬╪«╪╡ ╪ú╪│╪▒╪⌐ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪⌐ ┘ê╪º╪¡╪»╪⌐.
+    if (state.items.isNotEmpty &&
+        state.items.first.product.sellerId != product.sellerId) {
+      emit(
+        state.copyWith(
+          message:
+              '╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐ ╪¬╪¡╪¬┘ê┘è ╪╣┘ä┘ë ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪ú╪│╪▒╪⌐ ╪ú╪«╪▒┘ë. '
+              '╪ú┘ü╪▒╪║ ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐ ╪ú┘ê┘ä┘ï╪º ┘ä╪Ñ╪╢╪º┘ü╪⌐ ┘ç╪░╪º ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼.',
+        ),
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+
+    final items = List<CartItem>.from(state.items);
+
+    final existingIndex = items.indexWhere(
+      (item) => item.product.id == product.id,
+    );
+
+    if (existingIndex != -1) {
+      final existingItem = items[existingIndex];
+
+      items[existingIndex] = existingItem.copyWith(
+        quantity: existingItem.quantity + 1,
+      );
+    } else {
+      items.add(
+        CartItem(product: product),
+      );
+    }
+
+    emit(
+      state.copyWith(
+        items: items,
+        message: '╪¬┘à╪¬ ╪Ñ╪╢╪º┘ü╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪Ñ┘ä┘ë ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐.',
+      ),
+    );
+
+    await _persistItems(items, emit);
+  }
+
+  /// ╪▓┘è╪º╪»╪⌐ ┘â┘à┘è╪⌐ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼.
+  Future<void> _onQuantityIncreased(
+    CartQuantityIncreased event,
+    Emitter<CartState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    final items = List<CartItem>.from(state.items);
+
+    final index = items.indexWhere(
+      (item) => item.product.id == event.productId,
+    );
+
+    if (index == -1) return;
+
+    items[index] = items[index].copyWith(
+      quantity: items[index].quantity + 1,
+    );
+
+    emit(
+      state.copyWith(
+        items: items,
+        message: null,
+      ),
+    );
+
+    await _persistItems(items, emit);
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¬┘é┘ä┘è┘ä ┘â┘à┘è╪⌐ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼.
+  Future<void> _onQuantityDecreased(
+    CartQuantityDecreased event,
+    Emitter<CartState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    final items = List<CartItem>.from(state.items);
+
+    final index = items.indexWhere(
+      (item) => item.product.id == event.productId,
+    );
+
+    if (index == -1) return;
+
+    if (items[index].quantity <= 1) {
+      items.removeAt(index);
+    } else {
+      items[index] = items[index].copyWith(
+        quantity: items[index].quantity - 1,
+      );
+    }
+
+    emit(
+      state.copyWith(
+        items: items,
+        message: null,
+      ),
+    );
+
+    await _persistItems(items, emit);
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¡╪░┘ü ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘à┘å ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐.
+  Future<void> _onProductRemoved(
+    CartProductRemoved event,
+    Emitter<CartState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    final items = state.items
+        .where(
+          (item) => item.product.id != event.productId,
+        )
+        .toList();
+
+    emit(
+      state.copyWith(
+        items: items,
+        message: null,
+      ),
+    );
+
+    await _persistItems(items, emit);
+  }
+
+  /// ╪Ñ┘ü╪▒╪º╪║ ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐ ╪¿╪º┘ä┘â╪º┘à┘ä.
+  Future<void> _onCleared(
+    CartCleared event,
+    Emitter<CartState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    emit(const CartState());
+
+    try {
+      await _cartLocalRepository.clear();
+    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      addError(error, stackTrace);
+
+      emit(
+        state.copyWith(
+          message: '╪¬┘à ╪Ñ┘ü╪▒╪º╪║ ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐ ┘à╪ñ┘é╪¬┘ï╪º╪î ┘ä┘â┘å ╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪¡╪░┘ü ╪¿┘è╪º┘å╪º╪¬┘ç╪º ╪º┘ä┘à╪¡┘ü┘ê╪╕╪⌐.',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+
+  /// ┘à╪│╪¡ ╪▒╪│╪º┘ä╪⌐ ╪º┘ä╪¬┘å╪¿┘è┘ç.
+  void _onMessageCleared(
+    CartMessageCleared event,
+    Emitter<CartState> emit,
+  ) {
+    emit(
+      state.copyWith(message: null),
+    );
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¡┘ü╪╕ ╪ú╪¡╪»╪½ ┘å╪│╪«╪⌐ ┘à┘å ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐ ┘à╪¡┘ä┘è┘ï╪º.
+  Future<void> _persistItems(
+    List<CartItem> items,
+    Emitter<CartState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    try {
+      await _cartLocalRepository.saveItems(items);
+    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      addError(error, stackTrace);
+
+      emit(
+        state.copyWith(
+          message: '╪¬╪║┘è╪▒╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐╪î ┘ä┘â┘å ╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪¡┘ü╪╕┘ç╪º ╪╣┘ä┘ë ╪º┘ä╪¼┘ç╪º╪▓.',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+}

diff --git a/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/cart/bloc/cart_state.dart b/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/cart/bloc/cart_state.dart

index 0000000..4deea97

@@ -0,0 +1,31 @@
+part of 'cart_bloc.dart';
+
+@freezed
+sealed class CartState with _$CartState {
+  const factory CartState({
+    @Default(<CartItem>[]) List<CartItem> items,
+    String? message,
+  }) = _CartState;
+}
+
+extension CartStateTotals on CartState {
+  int get totalItems {
+    return items.fold(
+      0,
+      (total, item) => total + item.quantity,
+    );
+  }
+
+  double get totalAmount {
+    return items.fold(
+      0.0,
+      (total, item) => total + (item.product.price * item.quantity),
+    );
+  }
+
+  int? get sellerId {
+    return items.isEmpty ? null : items.first.product.sellerId;
+  }
+
+  bool get isEmpty => items.isEmpty;
+}

+                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
+                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
+                  ),
+                ],
+              ),
+            ),
+          );
+        }
+
+        final bloc = context.read<CartBloc>();
+
+        return ListView.separated(
+          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
+          itemCount: state.items.length,
+          separatorBuilder: (_, _) => const SizedBox(height: 10),
+          itemBuilder: (context, index) {
+            final item = state.items[index];
+            final productId = item.product.id;
+
+            if (productId == null) {
+              return CartItemCard(
+                item: item,
+                onIncrease: () {},
+                onDecrease: () {},
+                onRemove: () {},
+              );
+            }
+
+            return CartItemCard(
+              item: item,
+              onIncrease: () {
+                bloc.add(
+                  CartEvent.quantityIncreased(
+                    productId: productId,
+                  ),
+                );
+              },
+              onDecrease: () {
+                bloc.add(
+                  CartEvent.quantityDecreased(
+                    productId: productId,
+                  ),
+                );
+              },
+              onRemove: () {
+                bloc.add(
+                  CartEvent.productRemoved(
+                    productId: productId,
+                  ),
+                );
+              },
+            );
+          },
+        );
+      },
+    );
+  }
+}

diff --git a/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/checkout/bloc/checkout_bloc.dart b/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/checkout/bloc/checkout_bloc.dart
new file mode 100644

+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/cart/models/cart_item.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/order_repository.dart';
+
+part 'checkout_event.dart';
+part 'checkout_state.dart';
+part 'checkout_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+class CheckoutBloc extends Bloc<CheckoutEvent, CheckoutState> {
+  CheckoutBloc(this._orderRepository) : super(const CheckoutState.initial()) {
+    on<CheckoutSubmitted>(_onSubmitted);
+  }
+
+  final OrderRepository _orderRepository;
+
+  Future<void> _onSubmitted(
+    CheckoutSubmitted event,
+    Emitter<CheckoutState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    if (state.maybeWhen(
+      submitting: () => true,
+      orElse: () => false,
+    )) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    final address = event.deliveryAddress.trim();
+
+    if (address.isEmpty) {
+      emit(
+        const CheckoutState.failure(
+          '┘è╪▒╪¼┘ë ╪Ñ╪»╪«╪º┘ä ╪╣┘å┘ê╪º┘å ╪º┘ä╪¬┘ê╪╡┘è┘ä.',
+        ),
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (event.items.isEmpty) {
+      emit(
+        const CheckoutState.failure(
+          '╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐ ┘ü╪º╪▒╪║╪⌐╪î ╪ú╪╢┘ü ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪ú┘ê┘ä╪º┘ï.',
+        ),
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+
+    emit(const CheckoutState.submitting());
+
+    try {
+      final requestedItems = <OrderItemRequest>[];
+
+      for (final item in event.items) {
+        final productId = item.product.id;
+
+        if (productId == null || item.quantity <= 0) {
+          emit(
+            const CheckoutState.failure(
+              '╪¬┘ê╪¼╪» ╪¿┘è╪º┘å╪º╪¬ ╪║┘è╪▒ ╪╡╪¡┘è╪¡╪⌐ ┘ä╪ú╪¡╪» ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐.',
+            ),
+          );
+          return;
+        }
+
+        requestedItems.add(
+          OrderItemRequest(
+            productId: productId,
+            quantity: item.quantity,
+          ),
+        );
+      }
+
+      final result = await _orderRepository.createOrder(
+        items: requestedItems,
+        deliveryAddress: address,
+      );
+
+      result.fold(
+        (failure) {
+          emit(
+            CheckoutState.failure(failure.message),
+          );
+        },
+        (order) {
+          emit(
+            CheckoutState.success(order),
+          );
+        },
+      );
+    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      addError(error, stackTrace);
+
+      emit(
+        const CheckoutState.failure(
+          '╪¡╪»╪½ ╪«╪╖╪ú ╪ú╪½┘å╪º╪í ╪Ñ╪▒╪│╪º┘ä ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿. ╪¡╪º┘ê┘ä ┘à╪▒╪⌐ ╪ú╪«╪▒┘ë.',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+}

+
+@freezed
+sealed class CheckoutEvent with _$CheckoutEvent {
+  const factory CheckoutEvent.submitted({
+    required List<CartItem> items,
+    required String deliveryAddress,
+  }) = CheckoutSubmitted;
+}
diff --git a/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/checkout/bloc/checkout_state.dart b/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/checkout/bloc/checkout_state.dart
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..d76b2f3

+part of 'checkout_bloc.dart';
+
+@freezed
+sealed class CheckoutState with _$CheckoutState {
+  const factory CheckoutState.initial() = CheckoutInitial;
+
+  const factory CheckoutState.submitting() = CheckoutSubmitting;
+
+  const factory CheckoutState.success(OrderInfo order) = CheckoutSuccess;
+
+  const factory CheckoutState.failure(String message) = CheckoutFailure;

diff --git a/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/checkout/screen/checkout_screen.dart b/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/checkout/screen/checkout_screen.dart

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/cart/bloc/cart_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/checkout/bloc/checkout_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/checkout/view/checkout_view.dart';
+
+class CheckoutScreen extends StatefulWidget {
+  const CheckoutScreen({super.key});
+
+  @override
+  State<CheckoutScreen> createState() => _CheckoutScreenState();
+}
+
+class _CheckoutScreenState extends State<CheckoutScreen> {
+  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
+  final _addressController = TextEditingController();
+
+  @override
+  void dispose() {
+    _addressController.dispose();
+    super.dispose();
+  }
+
+  void _submitOrder() {
+    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
+
+    final cartItems = context.read<CartBloc>().state.items;
+
+    if (cartItems.isEmpty) return;
+
+    context.read<CheckoutBloc>().add(
+      CheckoutEvent.submitted(
+        items: cartItems,
+        deliveryAddress: _addressController.text,
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    return BlocConsumer<CheckoutBloc, CheckoutState>(
+      listener: (context, state) {
+        state.whenOrNull(
+          success: (_) {
+            // ╪¬┘ü╪▒┘è╪║ ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐ ╪¿╪╣╪» ╪ú┘å ┘è╪ñ┘â╪» ╪º┘ä╪«╪º╪»┘à ╪Ñ┘å╪┤╪º╪í ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿.
+            context.read<CartBloc>().add(
+              const CartEvent.cleared(),
+            );
+          },
+          failure: (message) {
+            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
+              ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
+              ..showSnackBar(
+                SnackBar(
+                  content: Text(message),
+                  backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,
+                ),
+              );
+          },
+        );
+      },
+      builder: (context, state) {
+        final isSubmitting = state.maybeWhen(
+          submitting: () => true,
+          orElse: () => false,
+        );
+
+        return state.maybeWhen(
+          success: (order) {
+            return Scaffold(
+              appBar: AppBar(
+                automaticallyImplyLeading: false,
+                title: const Text('╪¬┘à ╪¬╪ú┘â┘è╪» ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿'),

+                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
+                    children: [
+                      Icon(
+                        Icons.check_circle_outline,
+                        size: 80,
+                        color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary,
+                      ),
+                      const SizedBox(height: 20),
+                      Text(
+                        '╪¬┘à ╪º╪│╪¬┘ä╪º┘à ╪╖┘ä╪¿┘â ╪¿┘å╪¼╪º╪¡',
+                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
+                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
+                      ),
+                      const SizedBox(height: 12),

+                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
+                      Text(
+                        '╪º┘ä╪Ñ╪¼┘à╪º┘ä┘è: ${order.totalAmount.toStringAsFixed(2)} ╪▒.╪│',
+                      ),
+                      const SizedBox(height: 28),
+                      SizedBox(
+                        width: double.infinity,
+                        child: FilledButton(
+                          onPressed: () {
+                            Navigator.of(context).pop();
+                          },
+                          child: const Text('╪º┘ä╪╣┘ê╪»╪⌐ ╪Ñ┘ä┘ë ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐'),
+                        ),
+                      ),
+                    ],
+                  ),
+                ),
+              ),
+            );
+          },
+          orElse: () {
+            return BlocBuilder<CartBloc, CartState>(
+              builder: (context, cartState) {
+                return Scaffold(
+                  appBar: AppBar(
+                    title: const Text(
+                      '╪Ñ╪¬┘à╪º┘à ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿',
+                      style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
+                    ),
+                  ),
+                  body: cartState.isEmpty
+                      ? const Center(
+                          child: Text('╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐ ┘ü╪º╪▒╪║╪⌐.'),
+                        )
+                      : CheckoutView(
+                          items: cartState.items,
+                          addressController: _addressController,
+                          formKey: _formKey,
+                        ),
+                  bottomNavigationBar: cartState.isEmpty
+                      ? null
+                      : SafeArea(
+                          top: false,
+                          child: Padding(
+                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
+                            child: FilledButton.icon(
+                              onPressed: isSubmitting ? null : _submitOrder,
+                              style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
+                                minimumSize: const Size.fromHeight(54),
+                              ),
+                              icon: isSubmitting
+                                  ? const SizedBox(
+                                      width: 20,
+                                      height: 20,
+                                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(
+                                        strokeWidth: 2,
+                                      ),
+                                    )
+                                  : const Icon(Icons.check_circle_outline),

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/cart/models/cart_item.dart';
+
+class CheckoutView extends StatelessWidget {
+  const CheckoutView({
+    super.key,
+    required this.items,
+    required this.addressController,
+    required this.formKey,
+  });
+
+  final List<CartItem> items;
+  final TextEditingController addressController;
+  final GlobalKey<FormState> formKey;
+
+  double get totalAmount {
+    return items.fold(
+      0.0,
+      (total, item) => total + item.product.price * item.quantity,
+    );
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    final theme = Theme.of(context);
+    final colors = theme.colorScheme;
+

+      key: formKey,

+        child: Column(
+          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
+          children: [
+            Text(
+              '╪╣┘å┘ê╪º┘å ╪º┘ä╪¬┘ê╪╡┘è┘ä',
+              style: theme.textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+              ),
+            ),
+            const SizedBox(height: 12),
+            TextFormField(
+              controller: addressController,
+              minLines: 2,
+              maxLines: 4,
+              textInputAction: TextInputAction.newline,
+              decoration: const InputDecoration(
+                hintText: '╪ú╪»╪«┘ä ╪╣┘å┘ê╪º┘å ╪º┘ä╪¬┘ê╪╡┘è┘ä ╪¿╪º┘ä╪¬┘ü╪╡┘è┘ä...',
+                prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.location_on_outlined),
+                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
+                alignLabelWithHint: true,
+              ),
+              validator: (value) {
+                if (value == null || value.trim().isEmpty) {
+                  return '╪╣┘å┘ê╪º┘å ╪º┘ä╪¬┘ê╪╡┘è┘ä ┘à╪╖┘ä┘ê╪¿.';
+                }
+
+                return null;
+              },
+            ),
+            const SizedBox(height: 28),
+            Text(
+              '┘à╪▒╪º╪¼╪╣╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬',
+              style: theme.textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+              ),
+            ),
+            const SizedBox(height: 12),
+            ...items.map(
+              (item) => Card(
+                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
+                child: ListTile(
+                  leading: const Icon(Icons.restaurant_outlined),
+                  title: Text(
+                    item.product.name,
+                    maxLines: 2,
+                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
+                  ),
+                  subtitle: Text(
+                    '${item.product.price.toStringAsFixed(2)} ╪▒.╪│ ├ù ${item.quantity}',

+                  trailing: Text(

+                    style: TextStyle(
+                      color: colors.primary,
+                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                    ),
+                  ),
+                ),
+              ),

+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/order_repository.dart';
+
+part 'customer_orders_event.dart';
+part 'customer_orders_state.dart';
+part 'customer_orders_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+class CustomerOrdersBloc
+    extends Bloc<CustomerOrdersEvent, CustomerOrdersState> {
+  CustomerOrdersBloc(this._orderRepository)
+    : super(const CustomerOrdersState.initial()) {
+    on<CustomerOrdersLoadRequested>(_onLoadRequested);
+    on<CustomerOrdersRefreshRequested>(_onRefreshRequested);
+  }
+
+  final OrderRepository _orderRepository;
+
+  Future<void> _onLoadRequested(
+    CustomerOrdersLoadRequested event,
+    Emitter<CustomerOrdersState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    await _loadOrders(emit);
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _onRefreshRequested(
+    CustomerOrdersRefreshRequested event,
+    Emitter<CustomerOrdersState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    await _loadOrders(emit);
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _loadOrders(
+    Emitter<CustomerOrdersState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    emit(const CustomerOrdersState.loading());
+
+    final result = await _orderRepository.getMyCustomerOrders();
+
+    result.fold(
+      (failure) {
+        emit(CustomerOrdersState.error(failure.message));
+      },
+      (orders) {
+        emit(CustomerOrdersState.loaded(orders: orders));
+      },
+    );
+  }
+}

+part of 'customer_orders_bloc.dart';
+
+@freezed
+sealed class CustomerOrdersEvent with _$CustomerOrdersEvent {
+  const factory CustomerOrdersEvent.loadRequested() =
+      CustomerOrdersLoadRequested;
+
+  const factory CustomerOrdersEvent.refreshRequested() =
+      CustomerOrdersRefreshRequested;

+part of 'customer_orders_bloc.dart';
+
+@freezed
+sealed class CustomerOrdersState with _$CustomerOrdersState {
+  const factory CustomerOrdersState.initial() = CustomerOrdersInitial;
+
+  const factory CustomerOrdersState.loading() = CustomerOrdersLoading;
+
+  const factory CustomerOrdersState.loaded({
+    required List<OrderInfo> orders,
+  }) = CustomerOrdersLoaded;
+
+  const factory CustomerOrdersState.error(String message) = CustomerOrdersError;

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+
+class CustomerOrdersView extends StatelessWidget {
+  const CustomerOrdersView({
+    super.key,
+    required this.orders,
+  });

+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {

+            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,

+            ],
+          ),
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+
+    return ListView.separated(
+      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
+      itemCount: orders.length,
+      separatorBuilder: (_, _) => const SizedBox(height: 12),
+      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
+        return _CustomerOrderCard(order: orders[index]);
+      },
+    );
+  }
+}
+
+class _CustomerOrderCard extends StatelessWidget {
+  const _CustomerOrderCard({
+    required this.order,
+  });
+
+  final OrderInfo order;
+
+  String _statusLabel(String status) {
+    return switch (status) {
+      'pending' => '╪¿╪º┘å╪¬╪╕╪º╪▒ ┘é╪¿┘ê┘ä ╪º┘ä╪ú╪│╪▒╪⌐',
+      'preparing' => '┘é┘è╪» ╪º┘ä╪¬╪¼┘ç┘è╪▓',
+      'ready' => '╪¼╪º┘ç╪▓ ┘ä┘ä╪¬┘ê╪╡┘è┘ä',
+      'delivering' => '╪¼╪º╪▒┘ì ╪º┘ä╪¬┘ê╪╡┘è┘ä',
+      'completed' => '┘à┘â╪¬┘à┘ä',
+      'cancelled' => '┘à┘ä╪║┘è',
+      _ => '╪¡╪º┘ä╪⌐ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿: $status',
+    };
+  }
+
+  Color _statusColor(
+    String status,
+    ColorScheme colors,
+  ) {
+    return switch (status) {
+      'pending' => colors.tertiary,
+      'preparing' => colors.primary,
+      'ready' => colors.secondary,
+      'delivering' => colors.primary,
+      'completed' => Colors.green,
+      'cancelled' => colors.error,
+      _ => colors.onSurfaceVariant,
+    };
+  }
+
+  String _formatDate(DateTime date) {
+    final localDate = date.toLocal();
+
+    final day = localDate.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
+    final month = localDate.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
+
+    return '$day/$month/${localDate.year}';
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    final theme = Theme.of(context);
+    final colors = theme.colorScheme;
+    final statusColor = _statusColor(order.status, colors);
+
+    return Card(
+      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
+      child: Padding(
+        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
+        child: Column(
+          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
+          children: [
+            Row(
+              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
+              children: [
+                Expanded(
+                  child: Text(
+                    '╪╖┘ä╪¿ ╪▒┘é┘à #${order.id ?? '-'}',
+                    style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
+                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                    ),
+                  ),
+                ),
+                const Icon(
+                  Icons.receipt_long_outlined,
+                  color: Colors.grey,
+                ),
+              ],
+            ),
+            const SizedBox(height: 8),
+            Text(
+              _formatDate(order.createdAt),
+              style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
+                color: colors.onSurfaceVariant,
+              ),
+            ),
+            const SizedBox(height: 16),
+            Row(
+              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
+              children: [
+                const Text('╪Ñ╪¼┘à╪º┘ä┘è ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿'),
+                Text(
+                  '${order.totalAmount.toStringAsFixed(2)} ╪▒.╪│',
+                  style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
+                    color: colors.primary,
+                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                  ),
+                ),
+              ],
+            ),
+            const SizedBox(height: 14),
+            Container(
+              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
+                horizontal: 12,
+                vertical: 7,
+              ),
+              decoration: BoxDecoration(
+                color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
+                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
+              ),
+              child: Text(
+                _statusLabel(order.status),
+                style: TextStyle(
+                  color: statusColor,
+                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
+                  fontSize: 12,
+                ),
+              ),
+            ),
+          ],
+        ),

+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/local/favorites_local_repository.dart';
+
+part 'favorites_event.dart';
+part 'favorites_state.dart';
+part 'favorites_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+class FavoritesBloc extends Bloc<FavoritesEvent, FavoritesState> {
+  FavoritesBloc(
+    this._repository, {
+    List<Product> initialFavorites = const [],
+  }) : super(
+         FavoritesState(
+           products: List.unmodifiable(initialFavorites),
+         ),
+       ) {
+    on<FavoriteProductToggled>(_onProductToggled);
+    on<FavoritesCleared>(_onCleared);
+    on<FavoritesMessageCleared>(_onMessageCleared);
+  }
+
+  final FavoritesLocalRepository _repository;
+
+  Future<void> _onProductToggled(
+    FavoriteProductToggled event,
+    Emitter<FavoritesState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    final product = event.product;
+
+    if (product.id == null) {
+      emit(
+        state.copyWith(
+          message: '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪Ñ╪╢╪º┘ü╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪Ñ┘ä┘ë ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐.',
+        ),
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+
+    final products = List<Product>.from(state.products);
+
+    final index = products.indexWhere(
+      (item) => item.id == product.id,
+    );
+
+    final message = index != -1
+        ? '╪¬┘à╪¬ ╪Ñ╪▓╪º┘ä╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘à┘å ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐.'
+        : '╪¬┘à╪¬ ╪Ñ╪╢╪º┘ü╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪Ñ┘ä┘ë ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐.';
+
+    if (index != -1) {
+      products.removeAt(index);
+    } else {
+      products.add(product);
+    }
+
+    emit(
+      state.copyWith(
+        products: products,
+        message: message,
+      ),
+    );
+
+    await _saveFavorites(products, emit);
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _onCleared(
+    FavoritesCleared event,
+    Emitter<FavoritesState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    emit(const FavoritesState());
+
+    try {
+      await _repository.clear();
+    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      addError(error, stackTrace);
+
+      emit(
+        state.copyWith(
+          message: '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪¡╪░┘ü ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¡┘ü┘ê╪╕╪⌐ ╪╣┘ä┘ë ╪º┘ä╪¼┘ç╪º╪▓.',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+
+  void _onMessageCleared(
+    FavoritesMessageCleared event,
+    Emitter<FavoritesState> emit,
+  ) {
+    emit(
+      state.copyWith(message: null),
+    );
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _saveFavorites(
+    List<Product> products,
+    Emitter<FavoritesState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    try {
+      await _repository.saveFavorites(products);
+    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      addError(error, stackTrace);
+
+      emit(
+        state.copyWith(
+          message: '╪¬┘à ╪¬╪¡╪»┘è╪½ ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐╪î ┘ä┘â┘å ╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪¡┘ü╪╕┘ç╪º ╪╣┘ä┘ë ╪º┘ä╪¼┘ç╪º╪▓.',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+}

+part of 'favorites_bloc.dart';
+
+@freezed
+sealed class FavoritesState with _$FavoritesState {
+  const factory FavoritesState({
+    @Default(<Product>[]) List<Product> products,
+    String? message,
+  }) = _FavoritesState;
+}
+
+extension FavoritesStateX on FavoritesState {
+  bool containsProduct(int? productId) {
+    if (productId == null) return false;
+
+    return products.any(
+      (product) => product.id == productId,
+    );
+  }
+
+  bool get isEmpty => products.isEmpty;
+
+  int get count => products.length;

+      builder: (dialogContext) => AlertDialog(
+        title: const Text('╪Ñ┘ü╪▒╪º╪║ ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐'),

+            child: const Text('╪Ñ┘ü╪▒╪º╪║'),
+          ),

+      ),

+          ..showSnackBar(
+            SnackBar(content: Text(state.message!)),
+          );

+        context.read<FavoritesBloc>().add(
+          const FavoritesEvent.messageCleared(),
+        );
+      },
+      builder: (context, state) {
+        return Scaffold(
+          appBar: AppBar(
+            title: const Text(
+              '╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐',

+                  onPressed: () => _confirmClear(context),
+                  icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_sweep_outlined),
+                ),
+            ],
+          ),
+          body: FavoritesView(

+        );
+      },
+    );

+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/product_repository.dart';
+
+part 'home_state.dart';
+part 'home_event.dart';
+part 'home_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+class HomeBloc extends Bloc<HomeEvent, HomeState> {
+  final ProductRepository _productRepository;
+
+  HomeBloc(this._productRepository) : super(const HomeState.initial()) {
+    on<HomeProductsRequested>(_onProductsRequested);
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¬╪¡┘à┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¬╪º╪¡╪⌐ ┘à┘å ╪º┘ä╪«╪º╪»┘à.
+  Future<void> _onProductsRequested(
+    HomeProductsRequested event,
+    Emitter<HomeState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    emit(const HomeState.loading());
+
+    try {
+      final result = await _productRepository.getAllAvailableProducts();
+
+      result.fold(
+        (failure) {
+          emit(HomeState.error(failure.message));
+        },
+        (products) {
+          emit(
+            HomeState.productsLoaded(products: products),
+          );
+        },
+      );
+    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      addError(error, stackTrace);
+
+      emit(
+        const HomeState.error(
+          '╪¡╪»╪½ ╪«╪╖╪ú ╪║┘è╪▒ ┘à╪¬┘ê┘é╪╣ ╪ú╪½┘å╪º╪í ╪¬╪¡┘à┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬.',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+}

diff --git a/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/main/bloc/main_bloc.dart b/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/main/bloc/main_bloc.dart
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..5cfa88d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/main/bloc/main_bloc.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,48 @@
+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+
+part 'main_event.dart';
+part 'main_state.dart';
+part 'main_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+class MainBloc extends Bloc<MainEvent, MainState> {
+  static const int pageCount = 4;
+
+  MainBloc()
+    : super(
+        const MainState(
+          currentPage: 0,
+          visitedPages: {0},
+        ),
+      ) {
+    on<_PageChanged>(_onPageChanged);
+  }
+
+  void _onPageChanged(
+    _PageChanged event,
+    Emitter<MainState> emit,
+  ) {
+    // ╪º┘ä╪¬╪¡┘é┘é ┘à┘å ╪╡╪¡╪⌐ ╪▒┘é┘à ╪º┘ä╪╡┘ü╪¡╪⌐.
+    if (event.page < 0 || event.page >= pageCount) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    // ┘ä╪º ╪¡╪º╪¼╪⌐ ┘ä╪Ñ╪╡╪»╪º╪▒ ╪¡╪º┘ä╪⌐ ╪¼╪»┘è╪»╪⌐ ╪Ñ╪░╪º ┘â╪º┘å╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪╡┘ü╪¡╪⌐ ┘å┘ü╪│┘ç╪º ┘à┘ü╪¬┘ê╪¡╪⌐.
+    if (event.page == state.currentPage) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    // ╪º┘ä╪º╪¡╪¬┘ü╪º╪╕ ╪¿╪º┘ä╪╡┘ü╪¡╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪¬┘è ╪│╪¿┘é ┘ä┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à ┘ü╪¬╪¡┘ç╪º.
+    final visitedPages = <int>{
+      ...state.visitedPages,
+      event.page,
+    };
+
+    emit(
+      state.copyWith(
+        currentPage: event.page,
+        visitedPages: visitedPages,
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+}

+part of 'main_bloc.dart';
+
+@freezed
+sealed class MainEvent with _$MainEvent {

+  const factory MainEvent.pageChanged(int page) = _PageChanged;

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+
+class NavigationItem extends StatelessWidget {
+  final String label;
+  final IconData icon;
+  final IconData activeIcon;
+  final bool selected;
+  final VoidCallback onTap;
+
+  const NavigationItem({
+    super.key,
+    required this.label,
+    required this.icon,
+    required this.activeIcon,
+    required this.selected,
+    required this.onTap,
+  });
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    final colors = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
+    final color = selected ? colors.primary : colors.onSurfaceVariant;
+
+    return Expanded(
+      child: InkWell(
+        onTap: onTap,
+        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
+        child: SizedBox(
+          height: 64,
+          child: Column(
+            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
+            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
+            children: [
+              Icon(
+                selected ? activeIcon : icon,
+                color: color,
+                size: 24,
+              ),
+              const SizedBox(height: 4),
+              Text(
+                label,
+                maxLines: 1,
+                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
+                style: TextStyle(
+                  fontSize: 11,
+                  color: color,
+                  fontWeight: selected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
+                ),
+              ),
+            ],
+          ),
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+  }

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/core/constant/app_route.dart';
+
+import 'package:meer_flutter/core/id/injection.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/account/bloc/account_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/account/screen/account_screen.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/cart/bloc/cart_bloc.dart';

+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/customer_orders/screen/customer_orders_screen.dart';

+
+class MainScreen extends StatefulWidget {
+  const MainScreen({super.key});
+
+  @override
+  State<MainScreen> createState() => _MainScreenState();
+}
+
+class _MainScreenState extends State<MainScreen> {
+  HomeBloc? _homeBloc;
+  // ╪º┘ä╪º╪¡╪¬┘ü╪º╪╕ ╪¿╪╣┘å╪º╪╡╪▒ ╪º┘ä╪╡┘ü╪¡╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪¬┘è ╪ú┘Å┘å╪┤╪ª╪¬ ╪¿╪º┘ä┘ü╪╣┘ä.
+  final Map<int, Widget> _pageCache = {};
+
+  CustomerOrdersBloc? _customerOrdersBloc;
+
+  // ╪Ñ┘å╪┤╪º╪í ╪º┘ä╪╡┘ü╪¡╪⌐ ╪╣┘å╪» ╪ú┘ê┘ä ╪▓┘è╪º╪▒╪⌐ ┘ü┘é╪╖.
+  Widget _createPage(int index) {
+    switch (index) {
+      case 0:
+        return BlocProvider<HomeBloc>(
+          key: const ValueKey('main-home-page'),
+          create: (_) {
+            final bloc = sl<HomeBloc>();
+            _homeBloc = bloc;
+
+            bloc.add(const HomeProductsRequested());
+
+            return bloc;
+          },
+          child: const HomeScreen(),
+        );
+
+      case 1:
+        return const FavoritesScreen(
+          key: ValueKey('favorites-page'),
+        );
+
+      case 2:
+        return BlocProvider<CustomerOrdersBloc>(
+          key: const ValueKey('customer-orders-page'),
+          create: (_) {
+            final bloc = sl<CustomerOrdersBloc>();
+            _customerOrdersBloc = bloc;
+
+            bloc.add(
+              const CustomerOrdersEvent.loadRequested(),
+            );
+
+            return bloc;
+          },
+          child: const CustomerOrdersScreen(),
+        );
+
+      case 3:
+        return BlocProvider<AccountBloc>(
+          key: const ValueKey('account-page'),
+          create: (_) => sl<AccountBloc>()
+            ..add(
+              const AccountEvent.loadRequested(),
+            ),
+          child: const AccountScreen(),
+        );
+
+      default:
+        return const SizedBox.shrink();
+    }
+  }
+
+  // ╪Ñ┘å╪┤╪º╪í ╪º┘ä╪╣┘å╪º╪╡╪▒ ╪º┘ä╪¬┘è ╪▓╪º╪▒┘ç╪º ╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à ┘ü┘é╪╖.
+  List<Widget> _buildPages(Set<int> visitedPages) {
+    return List<Widget>.generate(
+      MainBloc.pageCount,
+      (index) {
+        if (!visitedPages.contains(index)) {
+          return SizedBox.shrink(
+            key: ValueKey('unvisited-page-$index'),
+          );
+        }
+
+        // ╪º╪│╪¬╪«╪»╪º┘à ╪╣┘å╪╡╪▒ ╪º┘ä╪╡┘ü╪¡╪⌐ ┘å┘ü╪│┘ç ╪¿╪╣╪» ╪ú┘ê┘ä ╪Ñ┘å╪┤╪º╪ª┘ç.
+        return _pageCache.putIfAbsent(
+          index,
+          () => _createPage(index),
+        );
+      },
+    );
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    return BlocBuilder<MainBloc, MainState>(
+      builder: (context, state) {
+        return Scaffold(
+          floatingActionButton: BlocBuilder<CartBloc, CartState>(
+            builder: (context, cartState) {
+              return FloatingActionButton(
+                tooltip: '╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐',
+                onPressed: () {
+                  Navigator.of(context).pushNamed(
+                    AppRoute.cartScreen,
+                  );
+                },
+                shape: const CircleBorder(),
+                child: Stack(
+                  clipBehavior: Clip.none,
+                  alignment: Alignment.center,
+                  children: [
+                    const Icon(Icons.shopping_cart_outlined),
+
+                    if (cartState.totalItems > 0)
+                      Positioned(
+                        top: -10,
+                        right: -12,
+                        child: Container(
+                          constraints: const BoxConstraints(
+                            minWidth: 21,
+                            minHeight: 21,
+                          ),
+                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
+                            horizontal: 5,
+                            vertical: 2,
+                          ),
+                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
+                            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,
+                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
+                          ),
+                          child: Text(
+                            cartState.totalItems > 99
+                                ? '99+'
+                                : '${cartState.totalItems}',
+                            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
+                            style: TextStyle(
+                              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onError,
+                              fontSize: 11,
+                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                            ),
+                          ),
+                        ),
+                      ),
+                  ],
+                ),
+              );
+            },
+          ),
+          floatingActionButtonLocation:
+              FloatingActionButtonLocation.centerDocked,
+          bottomNavigationBar: MainBottomAppBar(
+            currentPage: state.currentPage,
+            onTap: (index) {
+              final mainBloc = context.read<MainBloc>();
+
+              if (index == 0 && mainBloc.state.currentPage != 0) {
+                _homeBloc?.add(
+                  const HomeProductsRequested(),
+                );
+              }
+
+              if (index == 2 && mainBloc.state.currentPage != 2) {
+                _customerOrdersBloc?.add(
+                  const CustomerOrdersEvent.refreshRequested(),
+                );
+              }
+
+              mainBloc.add(
+                MainEvent.pageChanged(index),
+              );
+            },
+          ),
+          body: IndexedStack(
+            index: state.currentPage,
+            children: _buildPages(state.visitedPages),
+          ),
+        );
+      },
+    );
+  }
+}

+class _PlaceholderPage extends StatelessWidget {

+            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
+          ),

+    );
+  }

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/core/constant/app_route.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/cart/bloc/cart_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/product_details/view/product_details_view.dart';
+
+class ProductDetailsScreen extends StatelessWidget {
+  const ProductDetailsScreen({
+    super.key,
+    required this.product,
+  });
+
+  final Product product;
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    return BlocListener<CartBloc, CartState>(
+      listenWhen: (previous, current) => current.message != null,
+      listener: (context, state) {
+        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
+          ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
+          ..showSnackBar(
+            SnackBar(
+              content: Text(state.message!),
+              behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
+              duration: const Duration(seconds: 2),
+            ),
+          );
+
+        context.read<CartBloc>().add(
+          const CartEvent.messageCleared(),
+        );
+      },
+      child: Scaffold(
+        appBar: AppBar(
+          title: const Text(
+            '╪¬┘ü╪º╪╡┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼',
+            style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
+          ),
+        ),
+        body: SafeArea(
+          child: ProductDetailsView(
+            product: product,
+            onSellerTap: () {
+              Navigator.of(context).pushReplacementNamed(
+                AppRoute.sellerDetailsScreen,
+                arguments: product.sellerId,
+              );
+            },
+          ),
+        ),
+
+        bottomNavigationBar: BlocBuilder<CartBloc, CartState>(
+          builder: (context, cartState) {
+            final matchingItems = cartState.items.where(
+              (item) => item.product.id == product.id,
+            );
+
+            final cartItem = matchingItems.isEmpty ? null : matchingItems.first;
+
+            final isAvailable = product.isAvailable;
+
+            return SafeArea(
+              top: false,
+              child: Padding(
+                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
+                child: Column(
+                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
+                  children: [
+                    if (cartItem == null)
+                      SizedBox(
+                        width: double.infinity,

+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/product_repository.dart';
+
+part 'product_form_event.dart';
+part 'product_form_state.dart';
+part 'product_form_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+class ProductFormBloc extends Bloc<ProductFormEvent, ProductFormState> {
+  ProductFormBloc(
+    this._productRepository, {
+    this.productToEdit,
+  }) : super(const ProductFormState.initial()) {
+    on<ProductFormSubmitted>(_onSubmitted);
+  }
+
+  final ProductRepository _productRepository;
+
+  /// null ╪¬╪╣┘å┘è ╪Ñ╪╢╪º┘ü╪⌐ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪î ┘ê┘ê╪¼┘ê╪» ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘è╪╣┘å┘è ╪¬╪╣╪»┘è┘ä┘ç.
+  final Product? productToEdit;
+
+  Future<void> _onSubmitted(
+    ProductFormSubmitted event,
+    Emitter<ProductFormState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    final name = event.name.trim();
+    final price = double.tryParse(event.price.trim());
+    final description = event.description?.trim();
+    final imageUrl = event.imageUrl?.trim();
+
+    if (name.isEmpty) {
+      emit(
+        const ProductFormState.failure(
+          '┘è╪▒╪¼┘ë ╪Ñ╪»╪«╪º┘ä ╪º╪│┘à ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼.',
+        ),
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (price == null || !price.isFinite || price <= 0) {
+      emit(
+        const ProductFormState.failure(
+          '┘è╪▒╪¼┘ë ╪Ñ╪»╪«╪º┘ä ╪│╪╣╪▒ ╪╡╪¡┘è╪¡ ╪ú┘â╪¿╪▒ ┘à┘å ╪╡┘ü╪▒.',
+        ),
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (imageUrl != null && imageUrl.isNotEmpty) {
+      final uri = Uri.tryParse(imageUrl);
+
+      if (uri == null ||
+          !uri.hasAuthority ||
+          uri.host.isEmpty ||
+          !(uri.scheme == 'http' || uri.scheme == 'https')) {
+        emit(
+          const ProductFormState.failure(
+            '╪▒╪º╪¿╪╖ ╪º┘ä╪╡┘ê╪▒╪⌐ ╪║┘è╪▒ ╪╡╪º┘ä╪¡.',
+          ),
+        );
+        return;
+      }
+    }
+
+    emit(const ProductFormState.submitting());
+
+    try {
+      final existingProduct = productToEdit;
+
+      final product = Product(
+        id: existingProduct?.id,
+        sellerId: existingProduct?.sellerId ?? 0,
+        name: name,
+        description: description == null || description.isEmpty
+            ? null
+            : description,
+        price: price,
+        imageUrl: imageUrl == null || imageUrl.isEmpty ? null : imageUrl,
+        isAvailable: existingProduct?.isAvailable ?? true,
+        createdAt: existingProduct?.createdAt ?? DateTime.now().toUtc(),
+      );
+
+      final result = existingProduct == null
+          ? await _productRepository.addProduct(product)
+          : await _productRepository.updateProduct(product);
+
+      result.fold(
+        (failure) {
+          emit(ProductFormState.failure(failure.message));
+        },
+        (savedProduct) {
+          emit(ProductFormState.success(savedProduct));
+        },
+      );
+    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      addError(error, stackTrace);
+
+      emit(
+        ProductFormState.failure(
+          productToEdit == null
+              ? '╪¡╪»╪½ ╪«╪╖╪ú ╪ú╪½┘å╪º╪í ╪Ñ╪╢╪º┘ü╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼. ╪¡╪º┘ê┘ä ┘à╪▒╪⌐ ╪ú╪«╪▒┘ë.'
+              : '╪¡╪»╪½ ╪«╪╖╪ú ╪ú╪½┘å╪º╪í ╪¬╪╣╪»┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼. ╪¡╪º┘ê┘ä ┘à╪▒╪⌐ ╪ú╪«╪▒┘ë.',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+}

+@freezed
+sealed class ProductFormEvent with _$ProductFormEvent {
+  const factory ProductFormEvent.submitted({
+    required String name,
+    required String price,

+part of 'product_form_bloc.dart';
+
+@freezed
+sealed class ProductFormState with _$ProductFormState {
+  const factory ProductFormState.initial() = ProductFormInitial;
+
+  const factory ProductFormState.submitting() = ProductFormSubmitting;
+
+  const factory ProductFormState.success(Product product) = ProductFormSuccess;
+
+  const factory ProductFormState.failure(String message) = ProductFormFailure;

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/product_form/bloc/product_form_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/product_form/view/product_form_view.dart';
+
+class ProductFormScreen extends StatelessWidget {
+  const ProductFormScreen({
+    super.key,
+    this.productToEdit,
+  });
+
+  final Product? productToEdit;
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    return BlocConsumer<ProductFormBloc, ProductFormState>(
+      listener: (context, state) {
+        state.whenOrNull(
+          success: (_) {
+            Navigator.of(context).pop(true);
+          },
+          failure: (message) {
+            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
+              ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
+              ..showSnackBar(
+                SnackBar(
+                  content: Text(message),
+                  backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,
+                ),
+              );
+          },
+        );
+      },
+      builder: (context, state) {
+        final isSubmitting = state.maybeWhen(
+          submitting: () => true,
+          orElse: () => false,
+        );
+
+        return Scaffold(
+          appBar: AppBar(
+            title: Text(
+              productToEdit == null ? '╪Ñ╪╢╪º┘ü╪⌐ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼' : '╪¬╪╣╪»┘è┘ä ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼',
+              style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
+            ),
+          ),
+          body: SafeArea(
+            child: ProductFormView(
+              initialProduct: productToEdit,
+              isSubmitting: isSubmitting,
+              onSubmit:
+                  ({
+                    required name,
+                    required price,
+                    description,
+                    imageUrl,
+                  }) {
+                    context.read<ProductFormBloc>().add(
+                      ProductFormEvent.submitted(
+                        name: name,
+                        price: price,
+                        description: description,
+                        imageUrl: imageUrl,
+                      ),
+                    );
+                  },
+            ),
+          ),
+        );
+      },
+    );
+  }

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+
+typedef ProductFormSubmitCallback =
+    void Function({
+      required String name,
+      required String price,
+      String? description,
+      String? imageUrl,
+    });
+
+class ProductFormView extends StatefulWidget {
+  final Product? initialProduct;
+  const ProductFormView({
+    super.key,
+    required this.isSubmitting,
+    required this.onSubmit,
+    this.initialProduct,
+  });
+
+  final bool isSubmitting;
+  final ProductFormSubmitCallback onSubmit;
+
+  @override
+  State<ProductFormView> createState() => _ProductFormViewState();
+}
+
+class _ProductFormViewState extends State<ProductFormView> {
+  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
+
+  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
+  final _descriptionController = TextEditingController();
+  final _priceController = TextEditingController();
+  final _imageUrlController = TextEditingController();
+
+  @override
+  void initState() {
+    super.initState();
+
+    final product = widget.initialProduct;
+
+    if (product != null) {
+      _nameController.text = product.name;
+      _descriptionController.text = product.description ?? '';
+      _priceController.text = product.price.toString();
+      _imageUrlController.text = product.imageUrl ?? '';
+    }
+  }
+
+  @override
+  void dispose() {
+    _nameController.dispose();
+    _descriptionController.dispose();
+    _priceController.dispose();
+    _imageUrlController.dispose();
+    super.dispose();
+  }
+
+  void _submit() {
+    if (widget.isSubmitting) return;
+
+    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
+
+    widget.onSubmit(
+      name: _nameController.text,
+      price: _priceController.text,
+      description: _descriptionController.text,
+      imageUrl: _imageUrlController.text,
+    );
+  }
+
+  String? _validatePrice(String? value) {
+    if (value == null || value.trim().isEmpty) {
+      return '┘è╪▒╪¼┘ë ╪Ñ╪»╪«╪º┘ä ╪º┘ä╪│╪╣╪▒.';
+    }
+
+    final price = double.tryParse(value.trim());
+
+    if (price == null || !price.isFinite || price <= 0) {
+      return '╪ú╪»╪«┘ä ╪│╪╣╪▒┘ï╪º ╪╡╪¡┘è╪¡┘ï╪º ╪ú┘â╪¿╪▒ ┘à┘å ╪╡┘ü╪▒.';
+    }
+
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  String? _validateImageUrl(String? value) {
+    final text = value?.trim() ?? '';
+
+    if (text.isEmpty) return null;
+
+    final uri = Uri.tryParse(text);
+
+    if (uri == null ||
+        !uri.hasAuthority ||
+        uri.host.isEmpty ||
+        !(uri.scheme == 'http' || uri.scheme == 'https')) {
+      return '╪ú╪»╪«┘ä ╪▒╪º╪¿╪╖ ╪╡┘ê╪▒╪⌐ ┘è╪¿╪»╪ú ╪¿┘Ç https:// ╪ú┘ê http://';
+    }
+
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    return SingleChildScrollView(
+      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
+      child: Form(
+        key: _formKey,
+        child: Column(
+          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
+          children: [
+            Text(
+              widget.initialProduct == null
+                  ? '╪¿┘è╪º┘å╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪º┘ä╪¼╪»┘è╪»'
+                  : '╪¬╪╣╪»┘è┘ä ╪¿┘è╪º┘å╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼',
+              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+              ),
+            ),
+            const SizedBox(height: 8),
+            Text(
+              '╪ú╪»╪«┘ä ╪¿┘è╪º┘å╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪º┘ä╪¬┘è ╪│╪¬╪╕┘ç╪▒ ┘ä┘ä┘à╪┤╪¬╪▒┘è┘å.',
+              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
+            ),
+            const SizedBox(height: 24),
+
+            TextFormField(
+              controller: _nameController,
+              enabled: !widget.isSubmitting,
+              textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
+              decoration: const InputDecoration(
+                labelText: '╪º╪│┘à ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ *',
+                hintText: '┘à╪½╪º┘ä: ┘à╪╣┘à┘ê┘ä ╪¿╪º┘ä╪¬┘à╪▒',
+                prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.inventory_2_outlined),
+                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
+              ),
+              validator: (value) {
+                if (value == null || value.trim().isEmpty) {
+                  return '┘è╪▒╪¼┘ë ╪Ñ╪»╪«╪º┘ä ╪º╪│┘à ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼.';
+                }
+
+                return null;
+              },
+            ),
+
+            const SizedBox(height: 16),
+
+            TextFormField(
+              controller: _priceController,
+              enabled: !widget.isSubmitting,
+              keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
+                decimal: true,
+              ),
+              textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
+              decoration: const InputDecoration(
+                labelText: '╪º┘ä╪│╪╣╪▒ ╪¿╪º┘ä╪▒┘è╪º┘ä ╪º┘ä╪│╪╣┘ê╪»┘è *',
+                hintText: '┘à╪½╪º┘ä: 25.00',
+                prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.payments_outlined),
+                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
+              ),
+              validator: _validatePrice,
+            ),
+
+            const SizedBox(height: 16),
+
+            TextFormField(
+              controller: _descriptionController,
+              enabled: !widget.isSubmitting,
+              minLines: 3,
+              maxLines: 5,
+              textInputAction: TextInputAction.newline,
+              decoration: const InputDecoration(
+                labelText: '╪º┘ä┘ê╪╡┘ü (╪º╪«╪¬┘è╪º╪▒┘è)',
+                hintText: '╪º┘â╪¬╪¿ ┘ê╪╡┘ü┘ï╪º ┘à╪«╪¬╪╡╪▒┘ï╪º ┘ä┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼.',
+                prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.description_outlined),
+                alignLabelWithHint: true,
+                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
+              ),

diff --git a/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/search/bloc/search_bloc.dart b/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/search/bloc/search_bloc.dart
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..0369586

+++ b/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/search/bloc/search_bloc.dart
@@ -0,0 +1,61 @@
+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/search_repository.dart';
+
+part 'search_event.dart';
+part 'search_state.dart';
+part 'search_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+class SearchBloc extends Bloc<SearchEvent, SearchState> {
+  final SearchRepository searchRepository; // ╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪¬┘ê╪»╪╣ ╪º┘ä╪«╪º╪╡ ╪¿╪º┘ä╪¿╪¡╪½
+
+  SearchBloc(this.searchRepository) : super(const SearchState()) {
+    on<QueryChanged>(_onQueryChanged);
+    on<ClearSearch>(_onClearSearch);
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _onQueryChanged(
+    QueryChanged event,
+    Emitter<SearchState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    if (event.query.isEmpty) {
+      emit(
+        state.copyWith(status: const SearchStatus.initial(), searchResults: []),
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+
+    emit(state.copyWith(status: const SearchStatus.loading()));
+
+    final response = await searchRepository.searchResults(event.query);
+
+    response.fold(
+      (failure) =>
+          emit(state.copyWith(status: SearchStatus.failure(failure.message))),
+      (data) {
+        if (data.isEmpty) {
+          emit(
+            state.copyWith(
+              status: const SearchStatus.notFound(),
+              searchResults: [],
+            ),
+          );
+        } else {
+          emit(
+            state.copyWith(
+              status: const SearchStatus.loaded(),
+              searchResults: data,
+            ),
+          );
+        }
+      },
+    );
+  }
+
+  void _onClearSearch(ClearSearch event, Emitter<SearchState> emit) {
+    emit(
+      state.copyWith(status: const SearchStatus.initial(), searchResults: []),
+    );
+  }
+}

+  const factory SearchEvent.clearSearch() = ClearSearch;

+part of 'search_bloc.dart';
+
+@freezed
+class SearchStatus with _$SearchStatus {
+  const factory SearchStatus.initial() = _SearchInitial;
+  const factory SearchStatus.loading() = _SearchLoading;
+  const factory SearchStatus.loaded() = _SearchLoaded;
+  const factory SearchStatus.notFound() = _SearchNotFound;
+  const factory SearchStatus.failure(String errorMessage) = _SearchFailure;
+}
+
+@freezed
+abstract class SearchState with _$SearchState {
+  const factory SearchState({
+    @Default(SearchStatus.initial()) SearchStatus status,
+    @Default([]) List<Product> searchResults,
+  }) = _SearchState;
+}

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/search/bloc/search_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/search/components/list_products_search.dart';
+
+class CustomSearchDelegate extends SearchDelegate {
+  final SearchBloc searchBloc;
+
+  String? _lastRequestedQuery;
+
+  CustomSearchDelegate({
+    required this.searchBloc,
+  }) : super(
+         searchFieldLabel: '╪º╪¿╪¡╪½ ╪╣┘å ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬┘â ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐',
+       );
+
+  @override
+  List<Widget> buildActions(BuildContext context) {
+    return [
+      IconButton(
+        tooltip: '┘à╪│╪¡ ╪º┘ä╪¿╪¡╪½',
+        icon: const Icon(Icons.clear),
+        onPressed: () {
+          query = '';
+          _lastRequestedQuery = null;
+
+          searchBloc.add(
+            const SearchEvent.clearSearch(),
+          );
+
+          showSuggestions(context);
+        },
+      ),
+    ];
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget? buildLeading(BuildContext context) {
+    return IconButton(
+      tooltip: '╪▒╪¼┘ê╪╣',
+      icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
+      onPressed: () => close(context, null),
+    );
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget buildResults(BuildContext context) {
+    _searchIfNeeded();
+
+    return _buildSearchResultsAndStates();
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget buildSuggestions(BuildContext context) {
+    final currentQuery = query.trim();
+
+    if (currentQuery.isEmpty) {
+      return const Center(
+        child: Text('╪º╪¿╪¡╪½ ╪╣┘å ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬┘â ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐'),
+      );
+    }
+
+    // ┘ä╪º ╪¬╪╣╪▒╪╢ ┘å╪¬╪º╪ª╪¼ ╪º┘ä╪º╪│╪¬╪╣┘ä╪º┘à ╪º┘ä╪│╪º╪¿┘é ╪ú╪½┘å╪º╪í ┘â╪¬╪º╪¿╪⌐ ╪º╪│╪¬╪╣┘ä╪º┘à ╪¼╪»┘è╪».
+    if (currentQuery != _lastRequestedQuery) {
+      return const Center(
+        child: Text('╪º╪╢╪║╪╖ ╪¿╪¡╪½ ┘à┘å ┘ä┘ê╪¡╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪º╪¬┘è╪¡ ┘ä╪╣╪▒╪╢ ╪º┘ä┘å╪¬╪º╪ª╪¼'),
+      );
+    }
+
+    return _buildSearchResultsAndStates();
+  }
+
+  /// ╪Ñ╪▒╪│╪º┘ä ╪º╪│╪¬╪╣┘ä╪º┘à ┘ê╪º╪¡╪» ┘ä┘â┘ä ┘å╪╡ ╪¿╪¡╪½ ╪¼╪»┘è╪».
+  void _searchIfNeeded() {
+    final currentQuery = query.trim();
+
+    if (currentQuery.isEmpty) {
+      if (_lastRequestedQuery != '') {
+        _lastRequestedQuery = '';
+
+        searchBloc.add(
+          const SearchEvent.clearSearch(),
+        );
+      }
+
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (_lastRequestedQuery == currentQuery) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    _lastRequestedQuery = currentQuery;
+
+    searchBloc.add(
+      SearchEvent.queryChanged(currentQuery),
+    );
+  }
+
+  Widget _buildSearchResultsAndStates() {
+    return BlocBuilder<SearchBloc, SearchState>(
+      bloc: searchBloc,
+      builder: (context, state) {
+        return state.status.when(
+          initial: () => const Center(
+            child: Text('╪º╪¿╪¡╪½ ╪╣┘å ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬┘â ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐'),
+          ),
+          loading: () => const Center(
+            child: CircularProgressIndicator(),
+          ),
+          notFound: () => const Center(
+            child: Padding(
+              padding: EdgeInsets.all(24),
+              child: Column(
+                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
+                children: [
+                  Icon(
+                    Icons.search_off,
+                    size: 48,
+                  ),
+                  SizedBox(height: 12),
+                  Text(
+                    '┘ä┘à ┘å╪¼╪» ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪¬╪╖╪º╪¿┘é ╪¿╪¡╪½┘â.',
+                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
+                  ),
+                ],
+              ),
+            ),
+          ),
+          failure: (error) => Center(
+            child: Padding(
+              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
+              child: Text(
+                error,
+                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
+              ),
+            ),
+          ),
+          loaded: () => ListProductsSearch(
+            products: state.searchResults,
+          ),
+        );
+      },
+    );
+  }
+}

+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/product_repository.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/seller_repository.dart';
+
+part 'seller_details_event.dart';
+part 'seller_details_state.dart';
+part 'seller_details_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+class SellerDetailsBloc extends Bloc<SellerDetailsEvent, SellerDetailsState> {
+  SellerDetailsBloc(
+    this._sellerRepository,
+    this._productRepository,
+  ) : super(const SellerDetailsState.initial()) {
+    on<SellerDetailsLoadRequested>(_onLoadRequested);
+  }
+
+  final SellerRepository _sellerRepository;
+  final ProductRepository _productRepository;
+
+  Future<void> _onLoadRequested(
+    SellerDetailsLoadRequested event,
+    Emitter<SellerDetailsState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    emit(const SellerDetailsState.loading());
+
+    try {
+      final sellerResult = await _sellerRepository.getPublicSellerInfo(
+        sellerId: event.sellerId,
+      );
+
+      SellerPublicInfo? seller;
+      String? errorMessage;
+
+      sellerResult.fold(
+        (failure) {
+          errorMessage = failure.message;
+        },
+        (value) {
+          seller = value;
+        },
+      );
+
+      if (seller == null) {
+        emit(
+          SellerDetailsState.error(
+            errorMessage ?? '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪¬╪¡┘à┘è┘ä ┘à╪╣┘ä┘ê┘à╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪ú╪│╪▒╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪⌐.',
+          ),
+        );
+        return;
+      }
+
+      final productsResult = await _productRepository.getProductsBySeller(
+        event.sellerId,
+      );
+
+      List<Product>? products;
+
+      productsResult.fold(
+        (failure) {
+          errorMessage = failure.message;
+        },
+        (value) {
+          products = value;
+        },
+      );
+
+      if (products == null) {
+        emit(
+          SellerDetailsState.error(
+            errorMessage ?? '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪¬╪¡┘à┘è┘ä ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪ú╪│╪▒╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪⌐.',
+          ),
+        );
+        return;
+      }
+
+      emit(
+        SellerDetailsState.loaded(
+          seller: seller!,
+          products: products!,
+        ),
+      );
+    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      addError(error, stackTrace);
+
+      emit(
+        const SellerDetailsState.error(
+          '╪¡╪»╪½ ╪«╪╖╪ú ╪ú╪½┘å╪º╪í ╪¬╪¡┘à┘è┘ä ╪╡┘ü╪¡╪⌐ ╪º┘ä╪ú╪│╪▒╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪⌐.',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+}

+                  SizedBox(height: 12),
+                  Text('┘ä╪º ╪¬┘ê╪¼╪» ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ┘à╪¬╪º╪¡╪⌐ ┘ä┘ä╪¿┘è╪╣ ╪¡╪º┘ä┘è┘ï╪º.'),
+                ],
+              ),
+            )
+          else
+            LayoutBuilder(
+              builder: (context, constraints) {
+                final columns = constraints.maxWidth >= 650 ? 3 : 2;
+
+                return GridView.builder(
+                  shrinkWrap: true,
+                  physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
+                  itemCount: products.length,
+                  gridDelegate: SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
+                    crossAxisCount: columns,
+                    crossAxisSpacing: 12,
+                    mainAxisSpacing: 12,
+                    childAspectRatio: 0.68,
+                  ),
+                  itemBuilder: (context, index) {
+                    return ProductCard(
+                      product: products[index],
+                      onTap: () {
+                        Navigator.of(context).pushReplacementNamed(
+                          AppRoute.productDetailsScreen,
+                          arguments: products[index],
+                        );
+                      },
+                    );
+                  },
+                );
+              },
+            ),
+        ],
+      ),
+    );
+  }

diff --git a/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/seller_orders/bloc/seller_orders_bloc.dart b/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/seller_orders/bloc/seller_orders_bloc.dart

+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/order_repository.dart';
+
+part 'seller_orders_event.dart';
+part 'seller_orders_state.dart';
+part 'seller_orders_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+enum _SellerOrderAction {
+  accept,
+  reject,
+  markReady,
+}
+
+class SellerOrdersBloc extends Bloc<SellerOrdersEvent, SellerOrdersState> {
+  SellerOrdersBloc(this._orderRepository)
+    : super(const SellerOrdersState.initial()) {
+    on<SellerOrdersLoadRequested>(_onLoadRequested);
+    on<SellerOrdersRefreshRequested>(_onRefreshRequested);
+    on<SellerOrderAcceptRequested>(_onAcceptRequested);
+    on<SellerOrderRejectRequested>(_onRejectRequested);
+    on<SellerOrderMarkReadyRequested>(_onMarkReadyRequested);
+    on<SellerOrdersMessageCleared>(_onMessageCleared);
+  }
+
+  final OrderRepository _orderRepository;
+
+  Future<void> _onLoadRequested(
+    SellerOrdersLoadRequested event,
+    Emitter<SellerOrdersState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    await _loadOrders(emit);
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _onRefreshRequested(
+    SellerOrdersRefreshRequested event,
+    Emitter<SellerOrdersState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    await _loadOrders(emit);
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _loadOrders(
+    Emitter<SellerOrdersState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    emit(const SellerOrdersState.loading());
+
+    final result = await _orderRepository.getMySellerOrders();
+
+    result.fold(
+      (failure) {
+        emit(SellerOrdersState.error(failure.message));
+      },
+      (orders) {
+        emit(SellerOrdersState.loaded(orders: orders));
+      },
+    );
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _onAcceptRequested(
+    SellerOrderAcceptRequested event,
+    Emitter<SellerOrdersState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    await _performAction(
+      event.orderId,
+      _SellerOrderAction.accept,
+      emit,
+    );
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _onRejectRequested(
+    SellerOrderRejectRequested event,
+    Emitter<SellerOrdersState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    await _performAction(
+      event.orderId,
+      _SellerOrderAction.reject,
+      emit,
+    );
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _onMarkReadyRequested(
+    SellerOrderMarkReadyRequested event,
+    Emitter<SellerOrdersState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    await _performAction(
+      event.orderId,
+      _SellerOrderAction.markReady,
+      emit,
+    );
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _performAction(
+    int orderId,
+    _SellerOrderAction action,
+    Emitter<SellerOrdersState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    final currentOrders = _currentOrders();
+
+    if (currentOrders == null) return;
+
+    final currentUpdatingIds = _currentUpdatingIds();
+
+    if (currentUpdatingIds.contains(orderId)) return;
+
+    final orderExists = currentOrders.any(
+      (order) => order.id == orderId,
+    );
+
+    if (!orderExists) return;
+
+    emit(
+      SellerOrdersState.loaded(
+        orders: currentOrders,
+        updatingOrderIds: {
+          ...currentUpdatingIds,
+          orderId,
+        },
+      ),
+    );
+
+    final result = switch (action) {
+      _SellerOrderAction.accept => await _orderRepository.acceptMyOrder(
+        orderId,
+      ),
+      _SellerOrderAction.reject => await _orderRepository.rejectMyOrder(
+        orderId,
+      ),
+      _SellerOrderAction.markReady => await _orderRepository.markMyOrderReady(
+        orderId,
+      ),
+    };
+
+    await result.fold<Future<void>>(
+      (failure) async {
+        final latestOrders = _currentOrders() ?? currentOrders;
+        final latestUpdatingIds = _currentUpdatingIds()..remove(orderId);
+
+        emit(
+          SellerOrdersState.loaded(
+            orders: latestOrders,
+            updatingOrderIds: latestUpdatingIds,
+            message: failure.message,
+          ),
+        );
+      },
+      (_) async {
+        final refreshed = await _orderRepository.getMySellerOrders();
+
+        refreshed.fold(
+          (failure) {
+            final latestOrders = _currentOrders() ?? currentOrders;
+            final latestUpdatingIds = _currentUpdatingIds()..remove(orderId);
+
+            emit(
+              SellerOrdersState.loaded(
+                orders: latestOrders,
+                updatingOrderIds: latestUpdatingIds,
+                message:
+                    '╪¬┘à ╪¬╪¡╪»┘è╪½ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿╪î ┘ä┘â┘å ╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪¬╪¡╪»┘è╪½ ╪º┘ä┘é╪º╪ª┘à╪⌐: '
+                    '${failure.message}',
+              ),
+            );
+          },
+          (orders) {
+            final latestUpdatingIds = _currentUpdatingIds()..remove(orderId);
+
+            final message = switch (action) {
+              _SellerOrderAction.accept => '╪¬┘à ┘é╪¿┘ê┘ä ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿.',
+              _SellerOrderAction.reject => '╪¬┘à ╪▒┘ü╪╢ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿.',
+              _SellerOrderAction.markReady => '╪¬┘à ╪¬╪ú┘â┘è╪» ╪¼╪º┘ç╪▓┘è╪⌐ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿.',
+            };
+
+            emit(
+              SellerOrdersState.loaded(
+                orders: orders,
+                updatingOrderIds: latestUpdatingIds,
+                message: message,
+              ),
+            );
+          },
+        );
+      },
+    );
+  }
+
+  void _onMessageCleared(
+    SellerOrdersMessageCleared event,
+    Emitter<SellerOrdersState> emit,
+  ) {
+    state.maybeWhen(
+      loaded: (orders, updatingOrderIds, message) {
+        emit(
+          SellerOrdersState.loaded(
+            orders: orders,
+            updatingOrderIds: updatingOrderIds,
+          ),
+        );
+      },
+      orElse: () {},
+    );
+  }
+
+  List<OrderInfo>? _currentOrders() {
+    return state.maybeWhen(
+      loaded: (orders, updatingOrderIds, message) => orders,
+      orElse: () => null,
+    );
+  }
+
+  Set<int> _currentUpdatingIds() {
+    return state.maybeWhen(
+      loaded: (orders, updatingOrderIds, message) =>
+          Set<int>.from(updatingOrderIds),
+      orElse: () => <int>{},
+    );
+  }
+}

+@freezed
+sealed class SellerOrdersEvent with _$SellerOrdersEvent {

+

+      SellerOrdersRefreshRequested;

+part of 'seller_orders_bloc.dart';
+
+@freezed
+sealed class SellerOrdersState with _$SellerOrdersState {
+  const factory SellerOrdersState.initial() = SellerOrdersInitial;
+

+

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/seller_orders/bloc/seller_orders_bloc.dart';
