+class SellerOrdersScreen extends StatelessWidget {
+  const SellerOrdersScreen({super.key});
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    return BlocConsumer<SellerOrdersBloc, SellerOrdersState>(
+      listenWhen: (previous, current) {
+        final previousMessage = previous.maybeWhen(
+          loaded: (orders, updatingIds, message) => message,
+          orElse: () => null,
+        );
+
+        final currentMessage = current.maybeWhen(
+          loaded: (orders, updatingIds, message) => message,
+          orElse: () => null,
+        );
+
+        return currentMessage != null && currentMessage != previousMessage;
+      },
+      listener: (context, state) {
+        final message = state.maybeWhen(
+          loaded: (orders, updatingIds, message) => message,
+          orElse: () => null,
+        );
+
+        if (message == null) return;
+
+        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
+          ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
+          ..showSnackBar(
+            SnackBar(content: Text(message)),
+          );
+
+        context.read<SellerOrdersBloc>().add(
+          const SellerOrdersEvent.messageCleared(),
+        );
+      },
+      builder: (context, state) {
+        return Scaffold(
+          appBar: AppBar(
+            title: const Text(
+              '╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘ê╪º╪▒╪»╪⌐',
+              style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
+            ),
+            actions: [
+              IconButton(
+                tooltip: '╪¬╪¡╪»┘è╪½ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿╪º╪¬',
+                onPressed: () {
+                  context.read<SellerOrdersBloc>().add(
+                    const SellerOrdersEvent.refreshRequested(),
+                  );
+                },
+                icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
+              ),
+            ],
+          ),
+          body: state.when(
+            initial: () => const Center(
+              child: CircularProgressIndicator(),
+            ),
+            loading: () => const Center(
+              child: CircularProgressIndicator(),
+            ),
+            loaded: (orders, updatingIds, message) {
+              return SellerOrdersView(
+                orders: orders,
+                updatingOrderIds: updatingIds,
+                onAccept: (id) {
+                  context.read<SellerOrdersBloc>().add(
+                    SellerOrdersEvent.acceptRequested(orderId: id),
+                  );
+                },
+                onReject: (id) {
+                  context.read<SellerOrdersBloc>().add(
+                    SellerOrdersEvent.rejectRequested(orderId: id),
+                  );
+                },
+                onMarkReady: (id) {
+                  context.read<SellerOrdersBloc>().add(
+                    SellerOrdersEvent.markReadyRequested(orderId: id),
+                  );
+                },
+                onRefreshRequested: () {
+                  context.read<SellerOrdersBloc>().add(
+                    const SellerOrdersEvent.refreshRequested(),
+                  );
+                },
+              );
+            },
+            error: (message) => Center(
+              child: Padding(
+                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
+                child: Column(
+                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
+                  children: [
+                    const Icon(Icons.error_outline, size: 48),
+                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
+                    Text(
+                      message,
+                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
+                    ),
+                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
+                    FilledButton.icon(
+                      onPressed: () {
+                        context.read<SellerOrdersBloc>().add(
+                          const SellerOrdersEvent.loadRequested(),
+                        );
+                      },
+                      icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
+                      label: const Text('╪Ñ╪╣╪º╪»╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¡╪º┘ê┘ä╪⌐'),
+                    ),
+                  ],
+                ),
+              ),
+            ),
+          ),
+        );
+      },
+    );
+  }

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+
+class SellerOrderCard extends StatelessWidget {
+  const SellerOrderCard({
+    super.key,
+    required this.order,
+    required this.isUpdating,
+    required this.onAccept,
+    required this.onReject,
+    required this.onMarkReady,
+  });
+
+  final OrderInfo order;
+  final bool isUpdating;
+  final VoidCallback onAccept;
+  final VoidCallback onReject;
+  final VoidCallback onMarkReady;
+
+  String get _statusLabel {
+    return switch (order.status) {
+      'pending' => '╪╖┘ä╪¿ ╪¼╪»┘è╪»',
+      'preparing' => '┘é┘è╪» ╪º┘ä╪¬╪¼┘ç┘è╪▓',
+      'ready' => '╪¼╪º┘ç╪▓ ┘ä┘ä╪¬┘ê╪╡┘è┘ä',
+      'delivering' => '╪¼╪º╪▒┘ì ╪º┘ä╪¬┘ê╪╡┘è┘ä',
+      'completed' => '┘à┘â╪¬┘à┘ä',
+      'cancelled' => '┘à┘ä╪║┘è',
+      _ => order.status,
+    };
+  }
+
+  Color _statusColor(ColorScheme colors) {
+    return switch (order.status) {
+      'pending' => colors.tertiary,
+      'preparing' => colors.primary,
+      'ready' => Colors.green,
+      'delivering' => colors.secondary,
+      'completed' => Colors.green,
+      'cancelled' => colors.error,
+      _ => colors.onSurfaceVariant,
+    };
+  }
+
+  String _formatDate(DateTime date) {
+    final localDate = date.toLocal();
+
+    final day = localDate.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
+    final month = localDate.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
+
+    return '$day/$month/${localDate.year}';
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _confirmReject(BuildContext context) async {
+    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
+      context: context,
+      builder: (dialogContext) => AlertDialog(
+        title: const Text('╪▒┘ü╪╢ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿'),
+        content: Text(
+          '┘ç┘ä ╪ú┘å╪¬ ┘à╪¬╪ú┘â╪» ┘à┘å ╪▒┘ü╪╢ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿ ╪▒┘é┘à #${order.id}╪ƒ',
+        ),
+        actions: [
+          TextButton(
+            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialogContext, false),
+            child: const Text('╪Ñ┘ä╪║╪º╪í'),
+          ),
+          FilledButton(
+            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
+              backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,
+            ),
+            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialogContext, true),
+            child: const Text('╪▒┘ü╪╢ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿'),
+          ),
+        ],
+      ),
+    );
+
+    if (confirmed == true) {
+      onReject();
+    }
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    final theme = Theme.of(context);
+    final colors = theme.colorScheme;
+
+    final statusColor = _statusColor(colors);
+    final items = order.items ?? <OrderItem>[];
+
+    return Card(
+      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
+      child: Padding(
+        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
+        child: Column(
+          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
+          children: [
+            Row(
+              children: [
+                Expanded(
+                  child: Text(
+                    '╪╖┘ä╪¿ ╪▒┘é┘à #${order.id ?? '-'}',
+                    style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
+                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                    ),
+                  ),
+                ),
+                if (isUpdating)
+                  const SizedBox(
+                    width: 22,
+                    height: 22,
+                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(
+                      strokeWidth: 2,
+                    ),
+                  )
+                else
+                  Container(
+                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
+                      horizontal: 10,

+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/product_repository.dart';
+
+part 'seller_products_event.dart';
+part 'seller_products_state.dart';
+part 'seller_products_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+class SellerProductsBloc
+    extends Bloc<SellerProductsEvent, SellerProductsState> {
+  final ProductRepository _productRepository;
+
+  SellerProductsBloc(this._productRepository)
+    : super(const SellerProductsState.initial()) {
+    on<SellerProductsStarted>(_onStarted);
+    on<SellerProductsRefreshRequested>(_onRefreshRequested);
+    on<SellerProductAvailabilityChanged>(_onAvailabilityChanged);
+    on<SellerProductDeleteRequested>(_onDeleteRequested);
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¬╪¡┘à┘è┘ä ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪¿╪º╪ª╪╣ ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è.
+  Future<void> _onStarted(
+    SellerProductsStarted event,
+    Emitter<SellerProductsState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    await _loadProducts(emit);
+  }
+
+  /// ╪Ñ╪╣╪º╪»╪⌐ ╪¬╪¡┘à┘è┘ä ┘é╪º╪ª┘à╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬.
+  Future<void> _onRefreshRequested(
+    SellerProductsRefreshRequested event,
+    Emitter<SellerProductsState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    await _loadProducts(emit);
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _loadProducts(
+    Emitter<SellerProductsState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    emit(const SellerProductsState.loading());
+
+    try {
+      final result = await _productRepository.getMyProducts();
+
+      result.fold(
+        (failure) {
+          emit(SellerProductsState.error(failure.message));
+        },
+        (products) {
+          emit(
+            SellerProductsState.loaded(products: products),
+          );
+        },
+      );
+    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      addError(error, stackTrace);
+
+      emit(
+        const SellerProductsState.error(
+          '╪¡╪»╪½ ╪«╪╖╪ú ╪ú╪½┘å╪º╪í ╪¬╪¡┘à┘è┘ä ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ┘à╪¬╪¼╪▒┘â.',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¬╪║┘è┘è╪▒ ╪¬┘ê┘ü╪▒ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘è┘à┘ä┘â┘ç ╪º┘ä╪¿╪º╪ª╪╣ ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è.
+  Future<void> _onAvailabilityChanged(
+    SellerProductAvailabilityChanged event,
+    Emitter<SellerProductsState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    final currentProducts = state.maybeWhen(
+      loaded: (products, updatingProductIds, message) => products,
+      orElse: () => null,
+    );
+
+    if (currentProducts == null) return;
+
+    final currentUpdatingIds = state.maybeWhen(
+      loaded: (products, updatingProductIds, message) => updatingProductIds,
+      orElse: () => <int>{},
+    );
+
+    // ┘à┘å╪╣ ╪Ñ╪▒╪│╪º┘ä ╪ú┘â╪½╪▒ ┘à┘å ╪╖┘ä╪¿ ┘ä╪¬╪¡╪»┘è╪½ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘å┘ü╪│┘ç ╪¿╪º┘ä╪¬╪▓╪º┘à┘å.
+    if (currentUpdatingIds.contains(event.productId)) return;
+
+    emit(
+      SellerProductsState.loaded(
+        products: currentProducts,
+        updatingProductIds: {
+          ...currentUpdatingIds,
+          event.productId,
+        },
+      ),
+    );
+
+    try {
+      final result = await _productRepository.toggleProductAvailability(
+        productId: event.productId,
+        isAvailable: event.isAvailable,
+      );
+
+      await result.fold<Future<void>>(
+        (failure) async {
+          final latestProducts = _currentProducts() ?? currentProducts;
+          final latestUpdatingIds = _currentUpdatingIds()
+            ..remove(event.productId);
+
+          emit(
+            SellerProductsState.loaded(
+              products: latestProducts,
+              updatingProductIds: latestUpdatingIds,
+              message: failure.message,
+            ),
+          );
+        },
+        (_) async {
+          // ╪¼┘ä╪¿ ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘ü╪╣┘ä┘è╪⌐ ┘à┘å ╪º┘ä╪«╪º╪»┘à ╪¿╪╣╪» ┘å╪¼╪º╪¡ ╪º┘ä╪¬╪¡╪»┘è╪½.
+          final refreshed = await _productRepository.getMyProducts();
+
+          refreshed.fold(
+            (failure) {
+              final latestProducts = _currentProducts() ?? currentProducts;
+              final latestUpdatingIds = _currentUpdatingIds()
+                ..remove(event.productId);
+
+              emit(
+                SellerProductsState.loaded(
+                  products: latestProducts,
+                  updatingProductIds: latestUpdatingIds,
+                  message: failure.message,
+                ),
+              );
+            },
+            (products) {
+              final latestUpdatingIds = _currentUpdatingIds()
+                ..remove(event.productId);
+
+              emit(
+                SellerProductsState.loaded(
+                  products: products,
+                  updatingProductIds: latestUpdatingIds,
+                ),
+              );
+            },
+          );
+        },
+      );
+    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      addError(error, stackTrace);
+
+      final latestProducts = _currentProducts() ?? currentProducts;
+      final latestUpdatingIds = _currentUpdatingIds()..remove(event.productId);
+
+      emit(
+        SellerProductsState.loaded(
+          products: latestProducts,
+          updatingProductIds: latestUpdatingIds,
+          message: '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪¬╪¡╪»┘è╪½ ╪¡╪º┘ä╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼.',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¡╪░┘ü ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘è┘à┘ä┘â┘ç ╪º┘ä╪¿╪º╪ª╪╣ ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è.
+  Future<void> _onDeleteRequested(
+    SellerProductDeleteRequested event,
+    Emitter<SellerProductsState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    final currentProducts = _currentProducts();
+
+    // ┘ä╪º ┘è┘à┘â┘å ╪º┘ä╪¡╪░┘ü ╪Ñ╪░╪º ┘ä┘à ╪¬┘â┘å ╪º┘ä┘é╪º╪ª┘à╪⌐ ┘à╪¡┘à┘æ┘ä╪⌐.
+    if (currentProducts == null) return;
+
+    // ╪º┘ä╪¬╪ú┘â╪» ┘à┘å ┘ê╪¼┘ê╪» ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘ü┘è ╪º┘ä┘é╪º╪ª┘à╪⌐ ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è╪⌐.
+    final productExists = currentProducts.any(
+      (product) => product.id == event.productId,
+    );
+
+    if (!productExists) return;
+
+    final currentUpdatingIds = _currentUpdatingIds();
+
+    // ┘à┘å╪╣ ╪¬┘å┘ü┘è╪░ ╪╣┘à┘ä┘è╪¬┘è┘å ┘à╪¬╪▓╪º┘à┘å╪¬┘è┘å ╪╣┘ä┘ë ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘å┘ü╪│┘ç.
+    if (currentUpdatingIds.contains(event.productId)) return;
+
+    emit(
+      SellerProductsState.loaded(
+        products: currentProducts,
+        updatingProductIds: {
+          ...currentUpdatingIds,
+          event.productId,
+        },
+      ),
+    );
+
+    try {
+      final result = await _productRepository.deleteProduct(
+        event.productId,
+      );
+
+      result.fold(
+        (failure) {
+          final latestProducts = _currentProducts() ?? currentProducts;
+          final latestUpdatingIds = _currentUpdatingIds()
+            ..remove(event.productId);
+
+          emit(
+            SellerProductsState.loaded(
+              products: latestProducts,
+              updatingProductIds: latestUpdatingIds,
+              message: failure.message,
+            ),
+          );
+        },
+        (_) {
+          final latestProducts = _currentProducts() ?? currentProducts;
+          final latestUpdatingIds = _currentUpdatingIds()
+            ..remove(event.productId);
+
+          emit(
+            SellerProductsState.loaded(
+              products: latestProducts
+                  .where((product) => product.id != event.productId)
+                  .toList(),
+              updatingProductIds: latestUpdatingIds,
+            ),
+          );
+        },
+      );
+    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      addError(error, stackTrace);
+
+      final latestProducts = _currentProducts() ?? currentProducts;
+      final latestUpdatingIds = _currentUpdatingIds()..remove(event.productId);
+
+      emit(
+        SellerProductsState.loaded(
+          products: latestProducts,
+          updatingProductIds: latestUpdatingIds,
+          message: '╪¡╪»╪½ ╪«╪╖╪ú ╪ú╪½┘å╪º╪í ╪¡╪░┘ü ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼.',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+
+  List<Product>? _currentProducts() {
+    return state.maybeWhen(
+      loaded: (products, updatingProductIds, message) => products,
+      orElse: () => null,
+    );
+  }
+
+  Set<int> _currentUpdatingIds() {
+    return state.maybeWhen(
+      loaded: (products, updatingProductIds, message) =>
+          Set<int>.from(updatingProductIds),
+      orElse: () => <int>{},
+    );
+  }
+}

+@freezed
+sealed class SellerProductsEvent with _$SellerProductsEvent {

+
+  const factory SellerProductsEvent.refreshRequested() =
+      SellerProductsRefreshRequested;
+
+  const factory SellerProductsEvent.availabilityChanged({
+    required int productId,

+    required int productId,

+part of 'seller_products_bloc.dart';
+
+@freezed
+sealed class SellerProductsState with _$SellerProductsState {
+  const factory SellerProductsState.initial() = _SellerProductsInitial;
+
+  const factory SellerProductsState.loading() = _SellerProductsLoading;
+
+  const factory SellerProductsState.loaded({
+    required List<Product> products,
+    @Default(<int>{}) Set<int> updatingProductIds,
+    String? message,
+  }) = _SellerProductsLoaded;
+
+  const factory SellerProductsState.error(String message) =
+      _SellerProductsError;

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/core/constant/app_route.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/seller_products/bloc/seller_products_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/seller_products/view/seller_products_view.dart';
+
+class SellerProductsScreen extends StatelessWidget {
+  const SellerProductsScreen({super.key});
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    final colors = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
+
+    return Scaffold(
+      appBar: AppBar(
+        title: const Text(
+          '╪Ñ╪»╪º╪▒╪⌐ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬┘è',
+          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
+        ),
+        actions: [
+          IconButton(
+            tooltip: '╪¬╪¡╪»┘è╪½ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬',
+            onPressed: () {
+              context.read<SellerProductsBloc>().add(
+                const SellerProductsEvent.refreshRequested(),
+              );
+            },
+            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
+          ),
+        ],
+      ),
+      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
+        onPressed: () async {
+          final added = await Navigator.of(context).pushNamed<bool>(
+            AppRoute.productFormScreen,
+          );
+
+          if (!context.mounted) return;
+
+          if (added == true) {
+            context.read<SellerProductsBloc>().add(
+              const SellerProductsEvent.refreshRequested(),
+            );
+          }
+        },
+        icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
+        label: const Text('╪Ñ╪╢╪º┘ü╪⌐ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼'),
+      ),
+      body: BlocConsumer<SellerProductsBloc, SellerProductsState>(
+        listener: (context, state) {
+          state.whenOrNull(
+            loaded: (products, updatingProductIds, message) {
+              if (message == null) return;
+
+              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
+                SnackBar(
+                  content: Text(message),
+                  backgroundColor: colors.error,
+                ),
+              );
+            },
+          );
+        },
+        builder: (context, state) {
+          return state.maybeWhen(
+            initial: () => const Center(
+              child: CircularProgressIndicator(),
+            ),
+            loading: () => const Center(
+              child: CircularProgressIndicator(),
+            ),
+            loaded: (products, updatingProductIds, message) {
+              return SellerProductsView(
+                products: products,
+                updatingProductIds: updatingProductIds,
+                onAvailabilityChanged: (productId, isAvailable) {
+                  context.read<SellerProductsBloc>().add(
+                    SellerProductsEvent.availabilityChanged(
+                      productId: productId,
+                      isAvailable: isAvailable,
+                    ),
+                  );
+                },
+                onDeleteRequested: (productId) {
+                  context.read<SellerProductsBloc>().add(
+                    SellerProductsEvent.deleteRequested(
+                      productId: productId,
+                    ),
+                  );
+                },
+
+                onEdit: (product) async {
+                  final updated = await Navigator.of(context).pushNamed<bool>(
+                    AppRoute.productFormScreen,
+                    arguments: product,
+                  );
+
+                  if (!context.mounted) return;
+
+                  if (updated == true) {
+                    context.read<SellerProductsBloc>().add(
+                      const SellerProductsEvent.refreshRequested(),
+                    );
+                  }
+                },
+              );
+            },
+            error: (message) => Center(
+              child: Padding(
+                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
+                child: Column(
+                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
+                  children: [
+                    const Icon(Icons.error_outline, size: 48),
+                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
+                    Text(
+                      message,
+                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
+                    ),
+                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
+                    FilledButton.icon(
+                      onPressed: () {
+                        context.read<SellerProductsBloc>().add(
+                          const SellerProductsEvent.started(),
+                        );
+                      },
+                      icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
+                      label: const Text('╪Ñ╪╣╪º╪»╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¡╪º┘ê┘ä╪⌐'),
+                    ),
+                  ],
+                ),
+              ),
+            ),
+            orElse: () => const SizedBox.shrink(),
+          );
+        },
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+}

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/seller_products/view/widgets/seller_product_card.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/seller_products/view/widgets/seller_products_empty_state.dart';
+
+class SellerProductsView extends StatelessWidget {
+  final List<Product> products;
+  final Set<int> updatingProductIds;
+  final ValueChanged<Product> onEdit;
+  final ValueChanged<int> onDeleteRequested;
+  final void Function(int productId, bool isAvailable) onAvailabilityChanged;
+
+  const SellerProductsView({
+    super.key,
+    required this.products,
+    required this.updatingProductIds,
+    required this.onAvailabilityChanged,
+    required this.onEdit,
+    required this.onDeleteRequested,
+  });
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    final theme = Theme.of(context);
+    final colors = theme.colorScheme;
+
+    if (products.isEmpty) {
+      return SellerProductsEmptyState(theme: theme, colors: colors);
+    }
+
+    return ListView.separated(
+      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
+      itemCount: products.length,
+      separatorBuilder: (_, _) => const SizedBox(height: 10),
+      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
+        final product = products[index];
+        final isUpdating =
+            product.id != null && updatingProductIds.contains(product.id);
+
+        return SellerProductCard(
+          product: product,
+          theme: theme,
+          colors: colors,
+          isUpdating: isUpdating,
+          onAvailabilityChanged: onAvailabilityChanged,
+          onEdit: onEdit,
+          onDeleteRequested: onDeleteRequested,
+        );
+      },
+    );

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+
+Future<void> confirmDelete(
+  BuildContext context,
+  Product product,
+  ValueChanged<int> onDeleteRequested,
+) async {
+  final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
+    context: context,
+    builder: (dialogContext) => AlertDialog(
+      title: const Text('╪¡╪░┘ü ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼'),
+      content: Text(
+        '┘ç┘ä ╪ú┘å╪¬ ┘à╪¬╪ú┘â╪» ┘à┘å ╪¡╪░┘ü ┬½${product.name}┬╗╪ƒ '
+        '┘ä╪º ┘è┘à┘â┘å ╪º┘ä╪¬╪▒╪º╪¼╪╣ ╪╣┘å ┘ç╪░╪º ╪º┘ä╪Ñ╪¼╪▒╪º╪í.',
+      ),
+      actions: [
+        TextButton(
+          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialogContext, false),
+          child: const Text('╪Ñ┘ä╪║╪º╪í'),
+        ),
+        FilledButton(
+          style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
+            backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,
+          ),
+          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(dialogContext, true),
+          child: const Text('╪¡╪░┘ü'),
+        ),
+      ],
+    ),
+  );
+
+  if (confirmed == true && product.id != null) {
+    onDeleteRequested(product.id!);
+  }

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+
+class SellerProductImage extends StatelessWidget {
+  final Product product;
+  const SellerProductImage({super.key, required this.product});
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    return ClipRRect(
+      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
+      child: SizedBox(
+        width: 92,
+        height: 100,
+        child: _buildImage(product.imageUrl),
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+
+  Widget _buildImage(String? imageUrl) {
+    if (imageUrl == null || imageUrl.trim().isEmpty) {
+      return Container(
+        color: Colors.grey.shade200,
+        alignment: Alignment.center,
+        child: const Icon(
+          Icons.restaurant,
+          size: 36,
+          color: Colors.grey,
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+
+    return Image.network(
+      imageUrl,
+      fit: BoxFit.cover,
+      errorBuilder: (_, _, _) {
+        return Container(
+          color: Colors.grey.shade200,
+          alignment: Alignment.center,
+          child: const Icon(
+            Icons.image_not_supported_outlined,
+            size: 36,
+            color: Colors.grey,
+          ),
+        );
+      },
+    );
+  }
+}

diff --git a/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/seller_setup/bloc/seller_setup_bloc.dart b/meer_flutter/lib/presentation/seller_setup/bloc/seller_setup_bloc.dart

index 0000000..38fbf69

@@ -0,0 +1,51 @@
+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:freezed_annotation/freezed_annotation.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/repository/seller_repository.dart';
+
+part 'seller_setup_event.dart';
+part 'seller_setup_state.dart';
+part 'seller_setup_bloc.freezed.dart';
+
+class SellerSetupBloc extends Bloc<SellerSetupEvent, SellerSetupState> {
+  final SellerRepository _sellerRepository;
+
+  SellerSetupBloc(this._sellerRepository)
+    : super(const SellerSetupState.initial()) {
+    on<_SellerSetupSubmitted>(_onSubmitted);
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _onSubmitted(
+    _SellerSetupSubmitted event,
+    Emitter<SellerSetupState> emit,
+  ) async {
+    emit(const SellerSetupState.submitting());
+
+    try {
+      final result = await _sellerRepository.activateSeller(
+        storeName: event.storeName,
+        storeDescription: event.storeDescription,
+        storeAddress: event.storeAddress,
+        latitude: event.latitude,
+        longitude: event.longitude,
+      );
+
+      result.fold(
+        (failure) {
+          emit(SellerSetupState.failure(failure.message));
+        },
+        (profile) {
+          emit(SellerSetupState.success(profile: profile));
+        },
+      );
+    } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      addError(error, stackTrace);
+
+      emit(
+        const SellerSetupState.failure(
+          '╪¡╪»╪½ ╪«╪╖╪ú ╪║┘è╪▒ ┘à╪¬┘ê┘é╪╣ ╪ú╪½┘å╪º╪í ╪Ñ╪╣╪»╪º╪» ╪º┘ä┘à╪¬╪¼╪▒.',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+}

+@freezed

+  const factory SellerSetupEvent.submitted({
+    required String storeName,
+    required String? storeDescription,
+    required String storeAddress,
+    required double latitude,
+    required double longitude,
+  }) = _SellerSetupSubmitted;

+part of 'seller_setup_bloc.dart';
+
+@freezed
+sealed class SellerSetupState with _$SellerSetupState {
+  const factory SellerSetupState.initial() = _SellerSetupInitial;
+
+  const factory SellerSetupState.submitting() = _SellerSetupSubmitting;
+
+  const factory SellerSetupState.success({

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/core/constant/app_route.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/seller_setup/bloc/seller_setup_bloc.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/seller_setup/view/seller_setup_view.dart';
+
+class SellerSetupScreen extends StatefulWidget {
+  const SellerSetupScreen({super.key});
+
+  @override
+  State<SellerSetupScreen> createState() => _SellerSetupScreenState();
+}
+
+class _SellerSetupScreenState extends State<SellerSetupScreen> {
+  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
+
+  final _storeNameController = TextEditingController();
+  final _descriptionController = TextEditingController();
+  final _addressController = TextEditingController();
+  final _latitudeController = TextEditingController();
+  final _longitudeController = TextEditingController();
+
+  @override
+  void dispose() {
+    _storeNameController.dispose();
+    _descriptionController.dispose();
+    _addressController.dispose();
+    _latitudeController.dispose();
+    _longitudeController.dispose();
+    super.dispose();
+  }
+
+  void _submit() {
+    if (!(_formKey.currentState?.validate() ?? false)) {
+      return;
+    }
+
+    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
+
+    context.read<SellerSetupBloc>().add(
+      SellerSetupEvent.submitted(
+        storeName: _storeNameController.text.trim(),
+        storeDescription: _descriptionController.text.trim().isEmpty
+            ? null
+            : _descriptionController.text.trim(),
+        storeAddress: _addressController.text.trim(),
+        latitude: double.parse(
+          _latitudeController.text.trim(),
+        ),
+        longitude: double.parse(
+          _longitudeController.text.trim(),
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    final colors = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
+
+    return Scaffold(
+      appBar: AppBar(
+        title: const Text(
+          '╪Ñ╪╣╪»╪º╪» ╪º┘ä┘à╪¬╪¼╪▒',
+          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
+        ),
+      ),
+      body: BlocConsumer<SellerSetupBloc, SellerSetupState>(
+        listener: (context, state) {
+          state.whenOrNull(
+            failure: (message) {
+              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
+                SnackBar(
+                  content: Text(message),
+                  backgroundColor: colors.error,
+                ),
+              );
+            },
+            success: (profile) {
+              Navigator.pushReplacementNamed(
+                context,
+                AppRoute.sellerProductsScreen,
+              );
+            },
+          );
+        },
+        builder: (context, state) {
+          final isSubmitting = state.maybeWhen(
+            submitting: () => true,
+            orElse: () => false,
+          );
+
+          return SellerSetupView(
+            formKey: _formKey,
+            storeNameController: _storeNameController,
+            descriptionController: _descriptionController,
+            addressController: _addressController,
+            latitudeController: _latitudeController,
+            longitudeController: _longitudeController,
+            isSubmitting: isSubmitting,
+            onSubmit: _submit,
+          );
+        },
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+}

+class SellerSetupView extends StatelessWidget {

+

+    required this.latitudeController,
+    required this.longitudeController,
+    required this.isSubmitting,
+    required this.onSubmit,
+  });
+
+  String? _validateRequired(String? value, String fieldName) {
+    if (value == null || value.trim().isEmpty) {
+      return '$fieldName ┘à╪╖┘ä┘ê╪¿.';
+    }
+
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  String? _validateLatitude(String? value) {
+    final number = double.tryParse(value?.trim() ?? '');
+
+    if (number == null || !number.isFinite || number < -90 || number > 90) {
+      return '╪ú╪»╪«┘ä ╪«╪╖ ╪╣╪▒╪╢ ╪╡╪¡┘è╪¡┘ï╪º ╪¿┘è┘å -90 ┘ê90.';
+    }
+
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  String? _validateLongitude(String? value) {
+    final number = double.tryParse(value?.trim() ?? '');
+
+    if (number == null || !number.isFinite || number < -180 || number > 180) {
+      return '╪ú╪»╪«┘ä ╪«╪╖ ╪╖┘ê┘ä ╪╡╪¡┘è╪¡┘ï╪º ╪¿┘è┘å -180 ┘ê180.';
+    }
+
+    return null;
+  }

+            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
+            style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(

+            ),

+            enabled: !isSubmitting,
+            textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,

+              prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.store_outlined),

+  Widget _buildProductImage(String? imageUrl) {
+    final url = imageUrl?.trim();
+
+    if (url == null || url.isEmpty) {
+      return _imagePlaceholder();
+    }
+

+      url,
+      fit: BoxFit.cover,
+      errorBuilder: (_, _, _) => _imagePlaceholder(),
+    );
+  }

diff --git a/meer_flutter/lib/repository/local/cart_local_repository.dart b/meer_flutter/lib/repository/local/cart_local_repository.dart
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..066e881

+import 'package:hive_ce/hive_ce.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/presentation/cart/models/cart_item.dart';
+
+class CartLocalRepository {
+  CartLocalRepository(this._box);
+
+  final Box<dynamic> _box;
+
+  static const String _cartKey = 'items';
+
+  /// ╪º╪│╪¬╪╣╪º╪»╪⌐ ╪╣┘å╪º╪╡╪▒ ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐ ┘à┘å Hive.
+  Future<List<CartItem>> loadItems() async {
+    final storedItems = _box.get(_cartKey);
+
+    if (storedItems is! List) {
+      return [];
+    }
+
+    final restoredItems = <CartItem>[];
+
+    for (final rawItem in storedItems) {
+      if (rawItem is! Map) continue;
+
+      try {
+        final data = Map<String, dynamic>.from(rawItem);
+
+        final productId = data['id'];
+        final sellerId = data['sellerId'];
+        final quantity = data['quantity'];
+        final price = data['price'];
+        final createdAt = data['createdAt'];
+
+        if (productId is! int ||
+            sellerId is! int ||
+            quantity is! int ||
+            quantity <= 0 ||
+            price is! num ||
+            createdAt is! String) {
+          continue;
+        }
+
+        final product = Product(
+          id: productId,
+          sellerId: sellerId,
+          name: data['name'] as String,
+          description: data['description'] as String?,
+          price: price.toDouble(),
+          imageUrl: data['imageUrl'] as String?,
+          isAvailable: data['isAvailable'] as bool? ?? false,
+          createdAt: DateTime.parse(createdAt),
+        );
+
+        restoredItems.add(
+          CartItem(
+            product: product,
+            quantity: quantity,
+          ),
+        );
+      } catch (_) {
+        // ╪¬╪¼╪º┘ç┘ä ╪º┘ä╪╣┘å╪╡╪▒ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¡┘ä┘è ╪º┘ä╪¬╪º┘ä┘ü ╪»┘ê┘å ┘à┘å╪╣ ╪º╪│╪¬╪╣╪º╪»╪⌐ ╪¿┘é┘è╪⌐ ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐.
+        continue;
+      }
+    }
+
+    return restoredItems;
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¡┘ü╪╕ ╪╣┘å╪º╪╡╪▒ ╪º┘ä╪│┘ä╪⌐.
+  Future<void> saveItems(List<CartItem> items) async {
+    final data = items.map((item) {
+      final product = item.product;
+
+      return <String, dynamic>{
+        'id': product.id,
+        'sellerId': product.sellerId,
+        'name': product.name,
+        'description': product.description,
+        'price': product.price,
+        'imageUrl': product.imageUrl,
+        'isAvailable': product.isAvailable,
+        'createdAt': product.createdAt.toIso8601String(),
+        'quantity': item.quantity,
+      };
+    }).toList();
+
+    await _box.put(_cartKey, data);
+    await _box.flush();
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¡╪░┘ü ╪¼┘à┘è╪╣ ╪º┘ä╪╣┘å╪º╪╡╪▒ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¡┘ü┘ê╪╕╪⌐.
+  Future<void> clear() async {
+    await _box.delete(_cartKey);
+    await _box.flush();
+  }
+}

+import 'package:hive_ce/hive_ce.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+
+class FavoritesLocalRepository {
+  FavoritesLocalRepository(this._box);
+
+  final Box<dynamic> _box;
+
+  static const String _favoritesKey = 'products';
+
+  /// ╪º╪│╪¬╪╣╪º╪»╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐.
+  Future<List<Product>> loadFavorites() async {
+    final storedItems = _box.get(_favoritesKey);
+
+    if (storedItems is! List) return [];
+
+    final products = <Product>[];
+
+    for (final item in storedItems) {
+      if (item is! Map) continue;
+
+      try {
+        final data = Map<String, dynamic>.from(item);
+
+        final id = data['id'];
+        final sellerId = data['sellerId'];
+        final price = data['price'];
+        final name = data['name'];
+        final createdAt = data['createdAt'];
+
+        if (id is! int ||
+            sellerId is! int ||
+            price is! num ||
+            name is! String ||
+            createdAt is! String) {
+          continue;
+        }
+
+        products.add(
+          Product(
+            id: id,
+            sellerId: sellerId,
+            name: name,
+            description: data['description'] as String?,
+            price: price.toDouble(),
+            imageUrl: data['imageUrl'] as String?,
+            isAvailable: data['isAvailable'] as bool? ?? false,
+            createdAt: DateTime.parse(createdAt),
+          ),
+        );
+      } catch (_) {
+        // ╪¬╪¼╪º┘ê╪▓ ╪º┘ä╪¿┘è╪º┘å╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪¬╪º┘ä┘ü╪⌐ ┘à╪╣ ╪º┘ä╪º╪│╪¬┘à╪▒╪º╪▒ ┘ü┘è ╪º╪│╪¬╪╣╪º╪»╪⌐ ╪¿┘é┘è╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬.
+        continue;
+      }
+    }
+
+    return products;
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¡┘ü╪╕ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐.
+  Future<void> saveFavorites(List<Product> products) async {
+    final data = products.where((product) => product.id != null).map((product) {
+      return <String, dynamic>{
+        'id': product.id!,
+        'sellerId': product.sellerId,
+        'name': product.name,
+        'description': product.description,
+        'price': product.price,
+        'imageUrl': product.imageUrl,
+        'isAvailable': product.isAvailable,
+        'createdAt': product.createdAt.toIso8601String(),
+      };
+    }).toList();
+
+    await _box.put(_favoritesKey, data);
+    await _box.flush();
+  }
+
+  /// ╪Ñ┘ü╪▒╪º╪║ ╪º┘ä┘à┘ü╪╢┘ä╪⌐.
+  Future<void> clear() async {
+    await _box.delete(_favoritesKey);
+    await _box.flush();
+  }
+}

+import 'package:fpdart/fpdart.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/core/class/failure.dart';
+
+class OrderRepository {
+  /// ╪Ñ┘å╪┤╪º╪í ╪╖┘ä╪¿ ╪¼╪»┘è╪» ┘ä┘ä┘à╪┤╪¬╪▒┘è ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è.
+  Future<Either<Failure, OrderInfo>> createOrder({
+    required List<OrderItemRequest> items,
+    required String deliveryAddress,
+    double? deliveryLatitude,
+    double? deliveryLongitude,
+  }) async {
+    try {
+      final order = await client.order.createOrder(
+        items,
+        deliveryAddress,
+        deliveryLatitude,
+        deliveryLongitude,
+      );
+
+      return Right(order);
+    } catch (error) {
+      return Left(
+        ServerFailure(
+          '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪Ñ┘å╪┤╪º╪í ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿: ${error.toString()}',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¼┘ä╪¿ ╪╖┘ä╪¿╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à╪┤╪¬╪▒┘è ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è.
+  Future<Either<Failure, List<OrderInfo>>> getMyCustomerOrders() async {
+    try {
+      final orders = await client.order.getMyCustomerOrders();
+
+      return Right(orders);
+    } catch (error) {
+      return Left(
+        ServerFailure(
+          '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪¬╪¡┘à┘è┘ä ╪╖┘ä╪¿╪º╪¬┘â: ${error.toString()}',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¼┘ä╪¿ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘ê╪º╪▒╪»╪⌐ ┘ä┘ä╪¿╪º╪ª╪╣ ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è.
+  Future<Either<Failure, List<OrderInfo>>> getMySellerOrders() async {
+    try {
+      final orders = await client.order.getMySellerOrders();
+
+      return Right(orders);
+    } catch (error) {
+      return Left(
+        ServerFailure(
+          '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪¬╪¡┘à┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘ê╪º╪▒╪»╪⌐: ${error.toString()}',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+
+  /// ┘é╪¿┘ê┘ä ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿.
+  Future<Either<Failure, bool>> acceptMyOrder(
+    int orderId,
+  ) async {
+    return _executeOrderAction(
+      action: () => client.order.acceptMyOrder(orderId),
+      failureMessage: '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ┘é╪¿┘ê┘ä ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿.',
+    );
+  }
+
+  /// ╪▒┘ü╪╢ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿.
+  Future<Either<Failure, bool>> rejectMyOrder(
+    int orderId,
+  ) async {
+    return _executeOrderAction(
+      action: () => client.order.rejectMyOrder(orderId),
+      failureMessage: '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪▒┘ü╪╢ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿.',
+    );
+  }
+
+  /// ╪¬╪ú┘â┘è╪» ╪¼╪º┘ç╪▓┘è╪⌐ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿.
+  Future<Either<Failure, bool>> markMyOrderReady(
+    int orderId,
+  ) async {
+    return _executeOrderAction(
+      action: () => client.order.markMyOrderReady(orderId),
+      failureMessage: '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪¬╪¡╪»┘è╪½ ╪¡╪º┘ä╪⌐ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿.',
+    );
+  }
+
+  Future<Either<Failure, bool>> _executeOrderAction({
+    required Future<bool> Function() action,
+    required String failureMessage,
+  }) async {
+    try {
+      final success = await action();
+
+      if (!success) {
+        return Left(
+          ServerFailure(
+            '┘ä┘à ┘è╪¬┘à ╪¬╪¡╪»┘è╪½ ╪º┘ä╪╖┘ä╪¿. ╪▒╪¿┘à╪º ╪¬╪║┘è╪▒╪¬ ╪¡╪º┘ä╪¬┘ç ╪¿╪º┘ä┘ü╪╣┘ä.',
+          ),
+        );
+      }
+
+      return const Right(true);
+    } catch (error) {
+      return Left(
+        ServerFailure(
+          '$failureMessage ${error.toString()}',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }

+        product,

+        ServerFailure('╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪¬╪╣╪»┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼: ${e.toString()}'),

+import 'package:fpdart/fpdart.dart';
+import 'package:meer_client/meer_client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/client.dart';
+import 'package:meer_flutter/core/class/failure.dart';
+
+class SearchRepository {
+  /// ╪º┘ä╪¿╪¡╪½ ╪╣┘å ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¬╪º╪¡╪⌐.
+  Future<Either<Failure, List<Product>>> searchResults(
+    String search,
+  ) async {
+    final query = search.trim();
+
+    // ┘ä╪º ┘å╪▒╪│┘ä ╪╖┘ä╪¿┘ï╪º ╪Ñ┘ä┘ë ╪º┘ä╪«╪º╪»┘à ╪Ñ╪░╪º ┘â╪º┘å ╪º┘ä╪¿╪¡╪½ ┘ü╪º╪▒╪║┘ï╪º.
+    if (query.isEmpty) {
+      return Right(<Product>[]);
+    }
+
+    try {
+      final products = await client.product.searchProducts(
+        query,
+      );
+
+      return Right(products);
+    } catch (error) {
+      return Left(
+        ServerFailure(
+          '╪¬╪╣╪░╪▒ ╪º┘ä╪¿╪¡╪½ ╪╣┘å ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬: ${error.toString()}',
+        ),
+      );
+    }
+  }
+}

+                  'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1547592180-85f173990554?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80',
+            ),
+          ];
+
+      var inserted = 0;
+
+      for (final demo in demoProducts) {
+        // ┘å╪¬╪¡┘é┘é ┘à┘å ┘ê╪¼┘ê╪» ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪¿╪º┘ä╪º╪│┘à ╪»╪º╪«┘ä ╪º┘ä╪ú╪│╪▒╪⌐ ╪º┘ä╪¬╪¼╪▒┘è╪¿┘è╪⌐.
+        final existing = await Product.db.findFirstRow(
+          session,
+          where: (product) =>
+              product.sellerId.equals(sellerId) &
+              product.name.equals(demo.name),
+        );
+
+        if (existing != null) {
+          print('╪¬╪«╪╖┘è ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪º┘ä┘à┘ê╪¼┘ê╪»: ${demo.name}');
+          continue;
+        }
+
+        await Product.db.insertRow(
+          session,
+          Product(
+            sellerId: sellerId,
+            name: demo.name,
+            description: demo.description,
+            price: demo.price,
+            imageUrl: demo.imageUrl,
+            isAvailable: true,
+            createdAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
+          ),
+        );
+
+        inserted++;
+        print('╪¬┘à╪¬ ╪Ñ╪╢╪º┘ü╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼: ${demo.name}');
+      }
+
+      return inserted;
+    });
+
+    print('');
+    print('╪º┘â╪¬┘à┘ä Seeder ╪¿┘å╪¼╪º╪¡.');
+    print('╪╣╪»╪» ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪¼╪»┘è╪»╪⌐: $insertedCount');
+  } catch (error, stackTrace) {
+    stderr.writeln('┘ü╪┤┘ä ╪¬╪┤╪║┘è┘ä Seeder: $error');
+    stderr.writeln(stackTrace);
+    exitCode = 1;
+  } finally {
+    if (started) {
+      await pod.shutdown(exitProcess: false);
+    }
+  }
+}

+import 'package:meer_server/src/generated/protocol.dart';
+import 'package:serverpod/serverpod.dart';
+import 'package:serverpod_auth_idp_server/core.dart';
+
+/// ╪Ñ╪»╪º╪▒╪⌐ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪ú╪│╪▒ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪⌐ ┘ü┘è ╪¬╪╖╪¿┘è┘é ┘à┘É┘è┘Æ╪▒.
+class ProductEndpoint extends Endpoint {
+  /// ╪¼┘à┘è╪╣ ╪»┘ê╪º┘ä ┘ç╪░╪º ╪º┘ä┘Ç Endpoint ╪¬╪¬╪╖┘ä╪¿ ╪¬╪│╪¼┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä╪»╪«┘ê┘ä.
+  @override
+  bool get requireLogin => true;
+
+  /// ╪¼┘ä╪¿ ┘à┘ä┘ü ╪º┘ä╪¿╪º╪ª╪╣ ╪º┘ä┘à╪▒╪¬╪¿╪╖ ╪¿╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è.
+  ///
+  /// ┘ä╪º ┘å╪╣╪¬┘à╪» ╪╣┘ä┘ë sellerId ┘è╪▒╪│┘ä┘ç ╪¬╪╖╪¿┘è┘é Flutter╪î
+  /// ╪¿┘ä ┘å╪│╪¬╪«╪▒╪¼ ┘ç┘ê┘è╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à ┘à┘å ╪¼┘ä╪│╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à╪╡╪º╪»┘é╪⌐.
+  Future<MeerUserProfile> _getCurrentSellerProfile(
+    Session session,
+  ) async {
+    final authUserId = session.authenticated?.authUserId;
+
+    if (authUserId == null) {
+      throw Exception('┘è╪¼╪¿ ╪¬╪│╪¼┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä╪»╪«┘ê┘ä ╪ú┘ê┘ä╪º┘ï.');
+    }
+
+    final profile = await MeerUserProfile.db.findFirstRow(
+      session,
+      where: (p) => p.authUserId.equals(authUserId),
+    );
+
+    if (profile == null || profile.id == null) {
+      throw Exception(
+        '┘ä┘à ┘è╪¬┘à ╪º┘ä╪╣╪½┘ê╪▒ ╪╣┘ä┘ë ┘à┘ä┘ü ╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à. ╪ú┘â┘à┘ä ╪¿┘è╪º┘å╪º╪¬ ╪¡╪│╪º╪¿┘â ╪ú┘ê┘ä╪º┘ï.',
+      );
+    }
+
+    if (!profile.isSeller) {
+      throw Exception(
+        '┘è╪¼╪¿ ╪¬┘ü╪╣┘è┘ä ╪╡┘ä╪º╪¡┘è╪⌐ ╪º┘ä╪¿┘è╪╣ ┘é╪¿┘ä ╪Ñ╪»╪º╪▒╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬.',
+      );
+    }
+
+    return profile;
+  }
+
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+  // 1. ╪Ñ╪╢╪º┘ü╪⌐ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪¼╪»┘è╪»
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+
+  Future<Product> addProduct(
+    Session session,
+    Product product,
+  ) async {
+    final sellerProfile = await _getCurrentSellerProfile(session);
+
+    final productName = product.name.trim();
+
+    if (productName.isEmpty) {
+      throw FormatException(
+        '╪º╪│┘à ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘ä╪º ┘è┘à┘â┘å ╪ú┘å ┘è┘â┘ê┘å ┘ü╪º╪▒╪║╪º┘ï.',
+      );
+    }
+
+    if (!product.price.isFinite || product.price <= 0) {
+      throw FormatException(
+        '╪│╪╣╪▒ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘è╪¼╪¿ ╪ú┘å ┘è┘â┘ê┘å ╪ú┘â╪¿╪▒ ┘à┘å ╪╡┘ü╪▒.',
+      );
+    }
+
+    // ╪º┘ä╪«╪º╪»┘à ┘ç┘ê ╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪ñ┘ê┘ä ╪╣┘å ╪¬╪¡╪»┘è╪» ┘ç┘ê┘è╪⌐ ╪╡╪º╪¡╪¿ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼.
+    product.id = null;
+    product.sellerId = sellerProfile.id!;
+    product.name = productName;
+    product.createdAt = DateTime.now().toUtc();
+
+    return await Product.db.insertRow(
+      session,
+      product,
+    );
+  }
+
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+  // 2. ╪¼┘ä╪¿ ╪¼┘à┘è╪╣ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¬╪º╪¡╪⌐ ┘ä┘ä┘à╪┤╪¬╪▒┘è┘å
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+
+  Future<List<Product>> getAllAvailableProducts(
+    Session session,
+  ) async {
+    return await Product.db.find(
+      session,
+      where: (p) => p.isAvailable.equals(true),
+    );
+  }
+
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+  // 3. ╪¼┘ä╪¿ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¬╪º╪¡╪⌐ ┘ä╪ú╪│╪▒╪⌐ ┘à╪╣┘è┘å╪⌐
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+
+  Future<List<Product>> getProductsBySeller(
+    Session session,
+    int sellerId,
+  ) async {
+    return await Product.db.find(
+      session,
+      where: (p) => p.sellerId.equals(sellerId) & p.isAvailable.equals(true),
+    );
+  }
+
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+  // 4. ╪¼┘ä╪¿ ╪¼┘à┘è╪╣ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪¿╪º╪ª╪╣ ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è
+  //    ╪¿┘à╪º ┘ü┘è┘ç╪º ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ╪║┘è╪▒ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¬╪º╪¡╪⌐
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+
+  Future<List<Product>> getMyProducts(
+    Session session,
+  ) async {
+    final sellerProfile = await _getCurrentSellerProfile(session);
+
+    return await Product.db.find(
+      session,
+      where: (p) => p.sellerId.equals(sellerProfile.id!),
+    );
+  }
+
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+  // 5. ╪¬╪║┘è┘è╪▒ ╪¡╪º┘ä╪⌐ ╪¬┘ê┘ü╪▒ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼
+  //    ┘ä╪º ┘è╪│╪¬╪╖┘è╪╣ ╪º┘ä╪¿╪º╪ª╪╣ ╪¬╪╣╪»┘è┘ä ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪¿╪º╪ª╪╣ ╪ó╪«╪▒
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+
+  Future<bool> toggleProductAvailability(
+    Session session,
+    int productId,
+    bool isAvailable,
+  ) async {
+    final sellerProfile = await _getCurrentSellerProfile(session);
+
+    final product = await Product.db.findById(
+      session,
+      productId,
+    );
+
+    if (product == null) {
+      return false;
+    }
+
+    if (product.sellerId != sellerProfile.id) {
+      throw Exception(
+        '┘ä╪º ┘è┘à┘â┘å┘â ╪¬╪╣╪»┘è┘ä ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘ä╪º ╪¬┘à┘ä┘â┘ç.',
+      );
+    }
+
+    product.isAvailable = isAvailable;
+
+    await Product.db.updateRow(
+      session,
+      product,
+    );
+
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+  // 6. ╪º┘ä╪¿╪¡╪½ ┘ü┘è ╪ú╪│┘à╪º╪í ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ┘ê╪ú┘ê╪╡╪º┘ü┘ç╪º.
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+
+  /// ╪º┘ä╪¿╪¡╪½ ┘ü┘è ╪ú╪│┘à╪º╪í ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪º╪¬ ┘ê╪ú┘ê╪╡╪º┘ü┘ç╪º.
+  Future<List<Product>> searchProducts(
+    Session session,
+    String search,
+  ) async {
+    final query = search.trim();
+
+    if (query.isEmpty) {
+      return [];
+    }
+
+    final pattern = '%$query%';
+
+    return Product.db.find(
+      session,
+      where: (p) =>
+          p.isAvailable.equals(true) &
+          (p.name.ilike(pattern) | p.description.ilike(pattern)),
+    );
+  }
+
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+  /// 7. ╪¬╪╣╪»┘è┘ä ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+  Future<Product> updateProduct(
+    Session session,
+    Product input,
+  ) async {
+    final sellerProfile = await _getCurrentSellerProfile(session);
+
+    final productId = input.id;
+
+    if (productId == null) {
+      throw FormatException('┘à╪╣╪▒┘æ┘ü ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪║┘è╪▒ ╪╡╪º┘ä╪¡.');
+    }
+
+    final name = input.name.trim();
+    final price = input.price;
+
+    if (name.isEmpty) {
+      throw FormatException('╪º╪│┘à ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘ä╪º ┘è┘à┘â┘å ╪ú┘å ┘è┘â┘ê┘å ┘ü╪º╪▒╪║┘ï╪º.');
+    }
+
+    if (!price.isFinite || price <= 0) {
+      throw FormatException('┘è╪¼╪¿ ╪ú┘å ┘è┘â┘ê┘å ╪º┘ä╪│╪╣╪▒ ╪ú┘â╪¿╪▒ ┘à┘å ╪╡┘ü╪▒.');
+    }
+
+    final product = await Product.db.findById(
+      session,
+      productId,
+    );
+
+    if (product == null) {
+      throw Exception('╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ╪║┘è╪▒ ┘à┘ê╪¼┘ê╪».');
+    }
+
+    if (product.sellerId != sellerProfile.id) {
+      throw Exception('┘ä╪º ┘è┘à┘â┘å┘â ╪¬╪╣╪»┘è┘ä ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘ä╪º ╪¬┘à┘ä┘â┘ç.');
+    }
+
+    final description = input.description?.trim();
+    final imageUrl = input.imageUrl?.trim();
+
+    product.name = name;
+    product.description = description == null || description.isEmpty
+        ? null
+        : description;
+    product.price = price;
+    product.imageUrl = imageUrl == null || imageUrl.isEmpty ? null : imageUrl;
+
+    // ┘å╪¡╪º┘ü╪╕ ╪╣┘ä┘ë sellerId ┘êcreatedAt ┘êisAvailable ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è╪⌐.
+    return await Product.db.updateRow(session, product);
+  }
+
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+  /// 8. ╪¡╪░┘ü ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼
+  // --------------------------------------------------
+  Future<bool> deleteProduct(
+    Session session,
+    int productId,
+  ) async {
+    final sellerProfile = await _getCurrentSellerProfile(session);
+
+    final product = await Product.db.findById(
+      session,
+      productId,
+    );
+
+    if (product == null) {
+      return false;
+    }
+
+    if (product.sellerId != sellerProfile.id) {
+      throw Exception('┘ä╪º ┘è┘à┘â┘å┘â ╪¡╪░┘ü ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘ä╪º ╪¬┘à┘ä┘â┘ç.');
+    }
+
+    final existingOrderItem = await OrderItem.db.findFirstRow(
+      session,
+      where: (item) => item.productId.equals(productId),
+    );
+
+    if (existingOrderItem != null) {
+      throw Exception(
+        '┘ä╪º ┘è┘à┘â┘å ╪¡╪░┘ü ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼ ┘à╪▒╪¬╪¿╪╖ ╪¿╪╖┘ä╪¿ ╪│╪º╪¿┘é. ┘è┘à┘â┘å┘â ╪Ñ┘è┘é╪º┘ü ╪╣╪▒╪╢┘ç ╪¿╪»┘ä┘ï╪º ┘à┘å ╪░┘ä┘â.',
+      );
+    }
+
+    await Product.db.deleteRow(session, product);
+
+    return true;
+  }
+}

+import 'package:meer_server/src/generated/protocol.dart';
+import 'package:serverpod/serverpod.dart';
+import 'package:serverpod_auth_idp_server/core.dart';
+
+class SellerEndpoint extends Endpoint {
+  @override
+  bool get requireLogin => true;
+
+  // 1.
+  /// ╪¼┘ä╪¿ ╪º┘ä┘à╪╣┘ä┘ê┘à╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪╣╪º┘à╪⌐ ┘ä╪ú╪│╪▒╪⌐ ┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪⌐.
+  Future<SellerPublicInfo> getPublicSellerInfo(
+    Session session,
+    int sellerId,
+  ) async {
+    final profile = await MeerUserProfile.db.findById(
+      session,
+      sellerId,
+    );
+
+    if (profile == null || profile.id == null || !profile.isSeller) {
+      throw Exception('┘ä┘à ┘è╪¬┘à ╪º┘ä╪╣╪½┘ê╪▒ ╪╣┘ä┘ë ╪º┘ä╪ú╪│╪▒╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪⌐.');
+    }
+
+    final storeName = profile.storeName?.trim();
+
+    if (storeName == null || storeName.isEmpty) {
+      throw Exception('╪¿┘è╪º┘å╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä╪ú╪│╪▒╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪⌐ ╪║┘è╪▒ ┘à┘â╪¬┘à┘ä╪⌐.');
+    }
+
+    final description = profile.storeDescription?.trim();
+    final address = profile.storeAddress?.trim();
+
+    return SellerPublicInfo(
+      sellerId: profile.id!,
+      storeName: storeName,
+      storeDescription: description == null || description.isEmpty
+          ? null
+          : description,
+      storeAddress: address == null || address.isEmpty ? null : address,
+    );
+  }
+
+  // 2.
+  /// ╪¬┘ü╪╣┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä╪¿┘è╪╣ ┘ê╪¡┘ü╪╕ ╪¿┘è╪º┘å╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¬╪¼╪▒ ┘ä┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è.
+  Future<MeerUserProfile> activateSeller(
+    Session session,
+    String storeName,
+    String? storeDescription,
+    String storeAddress,
+    double latitude,
+    double longitude,
+  ) async {
+    // ╪º╪│╪¬╪«╪▒╪º╪¼ ┘ç┘ê┘è╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à ┘à┘å ╪¼┘ä╪│╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à╪╡╪º╪»┘é╪⌐.
+    final authUserId = session.authenticated?.authUserId;
+
+    if (authUserId == null) {
+      throw Exception('┘è╪¼╪¿ ╪¬╪│╪¼┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä╪»╪«┘ê┘ä ╪ú┘ê┘ä╪º┘ï.');
+    }
+
+    // ╪¼┘ä╪¿ ┘à┘ä┘ü ╪º┘ä╪¬╪╖╪¿┘è┘é ╪º┘ä┘à╪▒╪¬╪¿╪╖ ╪¿╪¡╪│╪º╪¿ ╪º┘ä┘à╪╡╪º╪»┘é╪⌐ ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è.
+    final profile = await MeerUserProfile.db.findFirstRow(
+      session,
+      where: (user) => user.authUserId.equals(authUserId),
+    );
+
+    if (profile == null || profile.id == null) {
+      throw Exception(
+        '┘ä┘à ┘è╪¬┘à ╪º┘ä╪╣╪½┘ê╪▒ ╪╣┘ä┘ë ┘à┘ä┘ü ╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à. ╪ú┘â┘à┘ä ╪¬╪│╪¼┘è┘ä ╪¡╪│╪º╪¿┘â ╪ú┘ê┘ä╪º┘ï.',
+      );
+    }
+
+    // ╪º┘ä╪¬╪¡┘é┘é ┘à┘å ╪º┘ä╪¿┘è╪º┘å╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à╪╖┘ä┘ê╪¿╪⌐.
+    final normalizedStoreName = storeName.trim();
+    final normalizedAddress = storeAddress.trim();
+
+    if (normalizedStoreName.isEmpty) {
+      throw FormatException('╪º╪│┘à ╪º┘ä╪ú╪│╪▒╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à┘å╪¬╪¼╪⌐ ┘à╪╖┘ä┘ê╪¿.');
+    }
+
+    if (normalizedAddress.isEmpty) {
+      throw FormatException('╪╣┘å┘ê╪º┘å ╪º┘ä┘à╪¬╪¼╪▒ ┘à╪╖┘ä┘ê╪¿.');
+    }
+
+    if (!latitude.isFinite || latitude < -90 || latitude > 90) {
+      throw FormatException('╪Ñ╪¡╪»╪º╪½┘è╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à┘ê┘é╪╣ ╪║┘è╪▒ ╪╡╪¡┘è╪¡╪⌐.');
+    }
+
+    if (!longitude.isFinite || longitude < -180 || longitude > 180) {
+      throw FormatException('╪Ñ╪¡╪»╪º╪½┘è╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à┘ê┘é╪╣ ╪║┘è╪▒ ╪╡╪¡┘è╪¡╪⌐.');
+    }
+
+    // ╪¡┘ü╪╕ ╪¿┘è╪º┘å╪º╪¬ ╪º┘ä┘à╪¬╪¼╪▒ ┘ü┘è ┘à┘ä┘ü ╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è.
+    profile.storeName = normalizedStoreName;
+    profile.storeDescription = storeDescription?.trim();
+    profile.storeAddress = normalizedAddress;
+    profile.storeLatitude = latitude;
+    profile.storeLongitude = longitude;
+
+    // ╪¬┘ü╪╣┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä╪¿┘è╪╣ ╪»┘ê┘å ╪¬╪║┘è┘è╪▒ ┘ç┘ê┘è╪⌐ ╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à ╪ú┘ê ╪¬╪╣╪╖┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä╪┤╪▒╪º╪í.
+    profile.isSeller = true;
+
+    return await MeerUserProfile.db.updateRow(
+      session,
+      profile,
+    );
+  }
+
+  // 3.
+  /// ╪¼┘ä╪¿ ┘à┘ä┘ü ╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à ╪º┘ä╪¡╪º┘ä┘è.
+  Future<MeerUserProfile> getMyProfile(
+    Session session,
+  ) async {
+    final authUserId = session.authenticated?.authUserId;
+
+    if (authUserId == null) {
+      throw Exception('┘è╪¼╪¿ ╪¬╪│╪¼┘è┘ä ╪º┘ä╪»╪«┘ê┘ä ╪ú┘ê┘ä╪º┘ï.');
+    }
+
+    final profile = await MeerUserProfile.db.findFirstRow(
+      session,
+      where: (user) => user.authUserId.equals(authUserId),
+    );
+
+    if (profile == null || profile.id == null) {
+      throw Exception(
+        '┘ä┘à ┘è╪¬┘à ╪º┘ä╪╣╪½┘ê╪▒ ╪╣┘ä┘ë ┘à┘ä┘ü ╪º┘ä┘à╪│╪¬╪«╪»┘à.',
+      );
+    }
+
+    return profile;
+  }

+BEGIN;
+
+--
+-- Function: gen_random_uuid_v7()
+-- Source: https://gist.github.com/kjmph/5bd772b2c2df145aa645b837da7eca74
+-- License: MIT (copyright notice included on the generator source code).
+--
+create or replace function gen_random_uuid_v7()
+returns uuid
+as $$
+begin
+  -- use random v4 uuid as starting point (which has the same variant we need)
+  -- then overlay timestamp
+  -- then set version 7 by flipping the 2 and 1 bit in the version 4 string
+  return encode(
+    set_bit(
+      set_bit(
+        overlay(uuid_send(gen_random_uuid())
+                placing substring(int8send(floor(extract(epoch from clock_timestamp()) * 1000)::bigint) from 3)
+                from 1 for 6
+        ),
+        52, 1
+      ),
+      53, 1
+    ),
+    'hex')::uuid;
+end
+$$
+language plpgsql
+volatile;
+
+--
+-- Class OrderInfo as table order_info
+--
+CREATE TABLE "order_info" (
+    "id" bigserial PRIMARY KEY,
+    "customerId" bigint NOT NULL,
+    "sellerId" bigint NOT NULL,
+    "driverId" bigint,
+    "status" text NOT NULL,
+    "totalAmount" double precision NOT NULL,
+    "deliveryAddress" text NOT NULL,
+    "deliveryLatitude" double precision,
+    "deliveryLongitude" double precision,
+    "createdAt" timestamp without time zone NOT NULL

+BEGIN;
+
+--
+-- Function: gen_random_uuid_v7()
+-- Source: https://gist.github.com/kjmph/5bd772b2c2df145aa645b837da7eca74
+-- License: MIT (copyright notice included on the generator source code).
+--
+create or replace function gen_random_uuid_v7()
+returns uuid
+as $$
+begin
+  -- use random v4 uuid as starting point (which has the same variant we need)
+  -- then overlay timestamp
+  -- then set version 7 by flipping the 2 and 1 bit in the version 4 string
+  return encode(
+    set_bit(
+      set_bit(
+        overlay(uuid_send(gen_random_uuid())
+                placing substring(int8send(floor(extract(epoch from clock_timestamp()) * 1000)::bigint) from 3)
+                from 1 for 6
+        ),
+        52, 1
+      ),
+      53, 1
+    ),
+    'hex')::uuid;
+end
+$$
+language plpgsql
+volatile;
+
